Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) from outcome rewards suffers from sparse supervision, particularly on difficult, long-horizon tasks where successful trajectories are rare and costly to generate. On-Policy Distillation (OPD) offers an attractive alternative by providing dense token-level supervision from a stronger teacher along the student's own generations. Self-distillation methods further remove the need for a separate teacher model by conditioning the same policy on privileged information to serve as its own teacher. However, privileged conditioning alone does not guarantee that the resulting distillation update improves the student. Indeed, privileged information can lead the teacher to solve tasks through shortcuts unavailable to the student, producing supervision poorly matched to the student's current behavior. Consequently, even a higher-performing teacher can provide guidance that degrades student performance. To address this, we analyze how the choice of privileged teacher affects the student's update. We derive a necessary and sufficient condition for the teacher's local distillation update to be a positive multiple of the student's reward gradient. Our analysis suggests that the teacher should not only perform well on the task, but also provide guidance suited to the student's current capabilities. This characterization motivates a practical teacher-training surrogate that combines outcome rewards with token-level Kullback-Leibler (KL) regularization toward the student. Based on this result, we propose Joint On-Policy Learning and Teaching (JOLT), which jointly trains a single policy in two roles: a privileged teacher using a KL-regularized objective, and an unprivileged student using dense on-policy distillation. Across mathematical reasoning, coding, tool use, and terminal use, JOLT improves training efficiency and performance, with further gains from student rewards.

</details>


### [30] [ResidualQuant: KV Cache Quantization for Looped Transformers with 2-Bit Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10381)
*Heejun Kim,Junyoung Lee,SangLyul Cho,Dongsu Han,Insu Han,Sehoon Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ResidualQuant 利用循环间 KV 高度相似的特性，以最终循环 KV 为参考，对残差进行低精度量化并结合最小二乘缩放、旋转及循环级混合精度，在 Looped Transformer 上实现 INT2 KV 量化。在数学推理和代码生成任务上，同显存预算下精度比旋转基线的 KV 量化高最多 13.0%，理论 KV 存储减少 80.7%，RTX 5090 上解码吞吐最高提升 2.73 倍，峰值吞吐最高提升 4.15 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Looped Transformer 的 KV Cache 随循环次数线性增长，成为限制批大小和吞吐的内存瓶颈；现有 KV 量化方法在极低比特（如 INT2）下精度退化严重。

Method: ResidualQuant 面向 Looped Transformer 的 KV Cache 压缩。Looped Transformer 将共享的 Transformer 块重复应用多个循环，KV 状态随循环次数累积，成为显存瓶颈。方法首先观察到跨循环的 KV 状态高度相似，因此选择最终循环的 KV 状态作为参考，其他循环的 KV 用低精度残差表示，从而将大部分存储移至低比特。为了在 INT2 等极低精度下保持精度，方法对残差施加最小二乘缩放和旋转，以降低量化误差；同时采用循环级混合精度，对不同循环分配不同比特宽度。推理时，通过参考 KV 和量化的残差重建各循环 KV，减少显存占用与内存流量，进而支持更大批次和更高吞吐。训练目标与优化细节未在摘要中披露。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "accuracy", "value": "up to 13.0% higher than rotation-based baseline", "setting": "same memory budget, mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, multiple looped Transformer models"}, {"metric": "theoretical KV storage reduction", "value": "80.7%", "setting": "mixed-precision settings, retaining accuracy close to BF16"}, {"metric": "fixed-batch decode throughput", "value": "up to 2.73x", "setting": "RTX 5090, reduced KV memory traffic"}, {"metric": "peak throughput", "value": "up to 4.15x", "setting": "RTX 5090, up to 2x larger batches enabled by smaller memory footprint"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 利用循环间 KV 状态高度相似性，以最终循环 KV 为参考，用低精度残差表示其余循环的 KV；对残差应用最小二乘缩放与旋转（least-square scaling and rotations）以提升量化精度；循环级混合精度（loop-wise mixed precision）策略，在 INT2 下保持接近 BF16 的精度并降低理论 KV 存储 80.7%；在 RTX 5090 上实现固定批次解码吞吐最高 2.73 倍提升，并支持 2 倍更大批次使峰值吞吐最高提升 4.15 倍

Abstract: Looped Transformers improve parameter efficiency by repeatedly applying shared Transformer blocks over multiple recurrent loops, increasing computational depth without increasing the parameter count. However, KV cache memory still scales with the number of loops, becoming a key memory bottleneck that limits batch size and inference throughput. KV cache quantization can alleviate this bottleneck, but existing methods often suffer substantial accuracy degradation at aggressive low-precision regimes. We observe that looped Transformers offer a unique opportunity: KV states across loops are highly similar. Based on this observation, we propose ResidualQuant, which uses the final-loop KV states as a reference and represents the remaining loops with low-precision residuals. Our method further combines least-square scaling and rotations applied to the residuals, as well as loop-wise mixed precision, to enable accurate quantization down to INT2 while retaining efficient reconstruction. Across multiple looped Transformer models and mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks, ResidualQuant consistently improves the accuracy-memory tradeoff over state-of-the-art rotation-based KV quantization. In particular, our method retains accuracy close to BF16 under mixed-precision settings while reducing theoretical KV storage by 80.7%, achieving up to 13.0% higher accuracy than the rotation-based baseline at the same memory budget. On an RTX 5090, the reduced KV memory traffic improves fixed-batch decode throughput by up to 2.73x, while the smaller memory footprint enables up to 2x larger batches, improving peak throughput by up to 4.15x.

</details>


### [31] [OrBIT: Structure-Guided Embedding Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10385)
*Yunied Puig,Amit Kumar Jaiswal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OrBIT提出一种结构引导的嵌入压缩框架，通过学习轨道动力学中的局部几何来约束共享码字，利用全局残差指导编码预算分配，并在重叠图上进行误差补偿，最终编译为紧凑解码器。在GPT-2和7B模型上分别实现37.9倍和超过23倍的压缩率，率失真性能与现有量化及低秩方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语言模型的嵌入表占据大量存储和内存，现有压缩方法固定编码几何（如坐标块、低秩子空间或无限制码本）并在其中优化，未探索编码几何本身的可学习性。

Method: OrBIT是一个结构引导的嵌入压缩框架。输入为LLM的嵌入表，输出为压缩后的紧凑解码器。首先，从嵌入表的轨道动力学中学习可复用的局部几何（紧致图），并以此几何约束一小部分共享码字。全局重构残差用于指导顺序分配有限的编码预算，决定哪些部分需要更多码字。冗余重叠的图（charts）允许局部误差在粘合阶段相互补偿，从而在全局重建时减小失真。训练目标是最小化重构误差，同时理论分析保证紧致图几何控制失真、全局残差引导分配、数据几何引导细化。推理时，轨道机制被编译消除，仅使用紧凑解码器根据学习到的结构重建嵌入向量。整个流程包括：学习局部几何 -> 共享码字约束 -> 全局残差分配 -> 重叠图误差补偿 -> 编译消除轨道机制 -> 紧凑解码器输出嵌入。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "压缩率", "value": "37.9×", "setting": "GPT-2嵌入表，相对于16比特存储"}, {"metric": "压缩率", "value": ">23×", "setting": "每个7B嵌入表，相对于16比特存储"}, {"metric": "率失真性能", "value": "具有竞争力", "setting": "与已有量化和低秩基线比较"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出OrBIT框架，从轨道动力学中学习可复用的局部几何，并用其约束一小部分共享码字。；利用全局重构残差决定固定编码预算的分配位置。；通过冗余重叠图（charts）使局部误差在粘合后相互补偿。；理论分析表明紧致图几何控制失真，全局残差指导顺序分配，数据几何引导的细化改善编解码器。；轨道机制被编译消除，仅留下紧凑解码器，由学习到的结构控制存储内容、容量分配及局部信息的全局组装。

Abstract: Embedding tables are among the largest components of modern language models. Most compression methods fix a coding geometry such as coordinate blocks, low-rank subspaces, or unrestricted codebooks, and optimize within it. We instead ask whether the coding geometry can itself be discovered. We introduce \emph{OrBIT}, a structure-guided embedding compression framework that learns reusable local geometry from orbit dynamics and uses it to constrain a small set of shared codewords. The global reconstruction residual then decides where the fixed coding budget is spent, while redundant overlapping charts let local errors compensate one another after gluing. Our theory shows how tight-chart geometry controls distortion, how the global residual directs sequential allocation, and how data-geometry-guided refinement improves the codec. The resulting orbit machinery is compiled away, leaving a compact decoder in which the learned structure governs what is stored, where capacity is allocated, and how local information is assembled globally. Across four LLM embedding tables, OrBIT achieves $37.9\times$ compression on GPT-2 and over $23\times$ on each 7B table relative to 16-bit storage, while delivering competitive rate-distortion performance against established quantization and low-rank baselines.

</details>

TL;DR: 论文提出EDR目标，通过马尔可夫奖励过程建模推测解码，直接最小化期望解码轮数，无需超参数，并给出无偏梯度与离线评估器，在九个基准上提升DSpark和DFly的平均接受长度，优于现有训练目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 论文将推测解码过程形式化为马尔可夫奖励过程：状态为当前解码轮起始位置及已接受token数，动作为草稿模型提出的token块，奖励为负的拒绝成本。基于此，EDR目标通过状态占用度对局部拒绝成本加权，精确等于期望解码轮数。训练时，从目标模型rollout中采样轨迹，利用推导的精确时序差分梯度进行无偏随机优化。该方法可直接微调现有并行/半自回归草稿模型（如DSpark、DFly），无需改变模型架构或引入额外超参数。推理时，草稿模型单次前向生成整个token块，目标模型并行验证，EDR训练促使草稿模型在全局轮数上更优。同时，同一框架提供离线评估器，可在给定目标模型rollout上精确计算解码轮数，支持配对比较。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates large language model inference by using a low-cost draft model to propose tokens that the full-size target model verifies in parallel. Parallel and semi-autoregressive (semi- AR) drafters improve drafting efficiency by proposing an entire block in a single forward pass, but training them raises a new difficulty: the draft distribution for a given position depends on where the decoding round starts, and where rounds start depends on how many tokens earlier rounds accepted. Existing training objectives typically rely on block-local surrogates that ignore this cross-round coupling, and therefore do not directly optimize the global decoding efficiency. In this work, we develop a theoretical framework for training and evaluating these drafters by representing speculative decoding as a Markov reward process. This formulation yields the Expected Decoding Rounds (EDR) objective, which weights local rejection costs by state occupancies and exactly equals the expected number of decoding rounds. Unlike prior surrogate objectives, EDR introduces no auxiliary hyperparameters. We then derive an exact temporal-difference gradient that supports unbiased stochastic optimization from target-model rollouts. The same framework also yields an exact offline evaluator for round counts, enabling paired drafter comparisons on shared target rollouts without running speculative decoding. Finetuning two state-of-the- art drafters, DSpark and DFly, with EDR consistently improves mean accepted length and outperforms existing training objectives across nine benchmarks spanning math reasoning, code generation, and chat.

</details>

Abstract: Instruction-tuned models are deployed into environments where domains are heterogeneous and evolve, yet adding new domains or data typically requires costly retraining. We present AutoAdapt, a modular framework that incorporates new domains and data via targeted single-adapter training without modifying other adapters. The framework automatically discovers latent domains, uses them to train per-domain Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) adapters independently in parallel and performs parameter-free routing. Across 14 domain-specific benchmarks and GPT-4o pairwise judgements, AutoAdapt achieves parity with a LoRA adapter trained on all domains without requiring full-model retraining. We also find evidence of specialisation effect convergence across independent discovery methods. Overall, training each adapter on its own domain prevents domain interference by construction, thus enabling modular, taxonomy-free domain specialisation without aggregate performance loss or full model retraining.

</details>

*Fanqing Meng,Lingxiao Du,Haocheng Lu,Qiguang Chen,Ziqi Zhao,Zijian Wu,Jiayuan Zhuo,Mengkang Hu,Michael Qizhe Shieh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RSIGym 是一个基于 Everything as a Service 的 agent-native 研究环境，把训练、推理、rollout、评估与沙箱包装成可复用服务，支持 Data、Harness、Joint 三类递归自我改进轨道。论文提出 RSI-Index 并在五个基准上比较六个前沿模型，Opus 5 在每基准 $500 预算下取得 0.4809，将 SWE-bench Verified 从 17.67% 提升到 50.33%、AIME 从 31.67% 提升到 97.78%，并开源完整代码与结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在可控预算和权限下，让 agent 递归地提出、执行并保留对数据、训练设置与执行 harness 的改进，同时避免每个研究周期重复搭建基础设施。

Method: RSIGym 以 Everything as a Service (EaaS) 为设计原则，将训练、推理、rollout、评估和沙箱执行封装为独立可复用服务，由共享的预算与权限控制层统一管理。环境提供 Data、Harness、Joint 三类改进轨道：Data 轨道允许 agent 修改训练或评测数据，Harness 轨道允许修改执行 harness，Joint 轨道允许联合优化数据、训练设置与执行 harness。Agent 在每轮中提出候选变更，通过服务调用执行训练/推理/rollout/评估，系统记录轨迹并依据预算与权限决定是否接受变更，被接受的变更带入后续改进周期。评测使用覆盖软件工程、终端交互、数学、科学推理和技能型任务的五个基准，RSI-Index 定义为各基准上剩余性能差距被关闭比例的平均值。实验比较六个前沿研究模型在独立 Joint 运行中的表现，并额外考察 DSH-harness 细化、预算变化与受限网络访问，轨迹用于分析 agent 如何诊断失败并选择候选。推理路径由服务编排，agent 决策与环境服务交互解耦，便于复现与扩展。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "RSI-Index", "value": "0.4809", "setting": "Opus 5 在独立 Joint 运行中，每个基准运行 $500 平台服务预算"}, {"metric": "SWE-bench Verified 准确率", "value": "从 17.67% 提升到 50.33%", "setting": "Opus 5 所选系统在所有五个基准上的提升之一"}, {"metric": "AIME 准确率", "value": "从 31.67% 提升到 97.78%", "setting": "Opus 5 所选系统在所有五个基准上的提升之一"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Everything as a Service (EaaS) 的 agent-native 研究环境 RSIGym，将训练、推理、rollout、评估与沙箱执行暴露为可复用服务；共享预算与权限控制，支持 Data、Harness、Joint 三类改进轨道，使 agent 能在同一环境内开展单组件干预与联合优化；提出 RSI-Index 指标，用五个基准上剩余性能差距被关闭的平均比例来度量递归自我改进效果；开源完整 RSIGym 代码库与结果，支持可复现研究

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement requires carrying accepted changes into later improvement cycles, while studying agent-proposed changes also requires substantial research infrastructure. Existing settings often leave agents to rebuild routine infrastructure or restrict exploration to individual components. We introduce RSIGym, an agent-native research environment based on Everything as a Service (EaaS). RSIGym exposes training, inference, rollout, evaluation, and sandbox execution through reusable services, with shared budget and permission controls supporting Data, Harness, and Joint improvement tracks. This design enables agents to investigate individual interventions and jointly optimize data, training settings, and execution harnesses within the same environment. We define RSI-Index as the mean fraction of the remaining performance gap closed across five benchmarks covering software engineering, terminal interaction, mathematics, scientific reasoning, and skill-based tasks. Comparing six frontier research models in independent Joint runs, Opus 5 achieves the highest RSI-Index of 0.4809 under a $500 platform-service budget per benchmark run. Its selected systems improve all five benchmarks, raising SWE-bench Verified from 17.67% to 50.33% and AIME from 31.67% to 97.78%. Additional experiments examine DSH-harness refinement, budget variation, and restricted network access, while recorded trajectories reveal how agents diagnose failures and select candidates. We open-source the full RSIGym codebase and results to support reproducibility and further research.

</details>

*Huishan Ji,Hua Xu,Weiming Zhang,Qirui Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: YANchor-4B是一个通用循环模型，其核心是维护一个多维记忆机制，将关键历史信息压缩存储为ANchors，并在后续推理步骤中通过检索机制访问这些ANchors。生成过程采用循环展开，每一步仅依赖当前输入和恒定大小的记忆状态，从而在O(N)时间内完成序列生成，且保持O(1)内存占用。训练目标未在摘要中明确说明，但模型在通用基准和数学推理任务上进行了评估。推理时，模型按照循环结构逐token生成，记忆更新和检索均在常数状态内完成，支持长程推理。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均pass@1", "value": "82.93%", "setting": "AIME 2024–2026"}, {"metric": "pass@1", "value": "63.64%", "setting": "HMMT"}, {"metric": "批量长生成吞吐量", "value": "数倍提升", "setting": "H100，对比Transformer和混合基线"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将关键记忆以ANchors形式保留供后续推理检索，实现O(N)时间与O(1)内存生成；多维记忆机制支持高效长程推理；在AIME 2024-2026、HMMT等数学推理基准上超越线性时间、常状态模型；在H100上实现比Transformer及混合基线高出数倍的批量长生成吞吐量

Abstract: Long-horizon reasoning demands access to earlier information at a manageable generation cost. Full-history attention incurs growing storage and computation, while recurrent compression can lose precise details. Therefore, we present YANchor-4B, a general-purpose recurrent model that preserves crucial memory as ANchors for retrieval during subsequent reasoning. Beyond $O(N)$-time generation and $O(1)$ memory, YANchor enables effective long-horizon reasoning through its multidimensional memory mechanism. For example, on challenging math problems, it achieves 82.93% mean pass@1 on AIME 2024--2026 and 63.64% on HMMT, substantially outperforming linear-time, constant-state counterparts, including larger models. It also delivers several-fold higher batched long-generation throughput than Transformer and hybrid baselines on H100. Furthermore, evaluations across dozens of benchmarks demonstrate YANchor's superiority in general-purpose capabilities.

</details>


### [36] [Activation-Aware Weight Tensorization: A Calibration-Time Preconditioner for Tensor-Network LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10085)
*Alessandro Beatini,Marco Maronese,Emanuele Rodolà*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AWT 提出一种激活感知的权重预条件方法，在不修改 TT/TTN 分解求解器的前提下，通过校准激活计算对角缩放，提升 LLM 张量网络压缩后的功能保真度，在 Llama 3.1 8B、Ministral 8B、Qwen2.5 7B 上显著缩小困惑度差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 TT/TTN 分解最小化权重空间 Frobenius 误差，而非在层激活分布下的功能误差，导致压缩后模型功能保真度不佳。

Method: AWT 是一种免训练的校准包装器，用于改进后训练张量网络压缩。输入为预训练 Transformer 的线性层权重矩阵和一小部分校准数据。首先，在校准数据上运行模型，收集每层输入的激活统计量，计算一个对角缩放矩阵，该矩阵反映激活各维度的尺度。然后，在将权重矩阵送入标准 TT/TTN 分解求解器之前，用该对角缩放对权重进行预条件。分解求解器保持不变，因此 AWT 模块化地提升功能保真度。部署时，压缩后的张量网络算子仅需在输入侧附加一个逐元素缩放操作，无需修改网络结构或分解求解器。训练目标仍由原求解器定义，AWT 通过改变分解前的权重空间来间接最小化激活分布下的功能误差。实验中在多个 LLM 家族和压缩率下验证了 WikiText 困惑度、HellaSwag 和 ARC-Challenge 的改善。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "WikiText 困惑度差距闭合比例", "value": "12-35%", "setting": "单算子替换，Llama 3.1 8B、Ministral 8B、Qwen2.5 7B，2-6 倍压缩"}, {"metric": "WikiText 困惑度差距闭合比例", "value": "27-60%", "setting": "多算子 Llama 后缀替换，注意力组和全部七个矩阵"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Activation-aware Weight Tensorization (AWT)：无训练校准包装器，在 TT/TTN 分解前用激活导出的对角缩放对权重矩阵进行预条件。；部署时仅需输入侧逐元素缩放，不改变原有分解求解器，保持模块化。；在 Llama 3.1 8B、Ministral 8B、Qwen2.5 7B 上验证，2-6 倍压缩下缩小 WikiText 困惑度差距。；揭示对角预条件是鲁棒性与模块性的权衡：全协方差 oracle 在其加权目标上胜出 80/81 次，但对角 AWT 在 53/81 次获得更好的留出功能保真度。

Abstract: Post-training tensor-network compression replaces Transformer linear layers with Tensor Train (TT) or Tree Tensor Network (TTN) operators, but standard decompositions minimize weight-space Frobenius error rather than functional error under the layer's activation distribution. We propose Activation-aware Weight Tensorization (AWT), a training-free calibration wrapper that preconditions each weight matrix with a diagonal activation-derived scale before an unchanged TT/TTN solver and deploys the result with only an input-side elementwise rescaling. Across Llama 3.1 8B, Ministral 8B, and Qwen2.5 7B, AWT consistently improves vanilla TT/TTN tensorization at 2-6 times compression: under single-operator replacement, AWT closes 12-35% of the WikiText perplexity gap to the dense baseline across the three model families and 2-6 times compression settings; while under multi-operator Llama suffix replacement it closes 27-60% across attention-group and all-seven-matrix settings. The gains also transfer to downstream HellaSwag and ARC-Challenge evaluations. We further show that diagonal preconditioning is a robustness-modularity tradeoff rather than a diagonal-covariance assumption: a dense full-covariance oracle wins its own weighted objective in 80/81 cases, yet diagonal AWT gives better held-out functional fidelity in 53/81 cases. Together, these results position AWT as a principled, modular preconditioner for improving functional fidelity in fixed TT/TTN compression pipelines without modifying the decomposition solver.

</details>


### [37] [TR-PTQ: High-Accuracy Integer-Only Transformer Post Training Quantization via Taylor Region Reformulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09969)
*Eliyahu Levy,Adam Teman,Yoni Pugachov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TR-PTQ 提出整数-only 的 Transformer 训练后量化方法，通过共享 Taylor Region 指数/对数原语在 log-domain 用整数算术实现除法与平方根等运算，并免校准优化 LayerNorm 参数，消除非线性对浮点硬件的依赖，在视觉与语言基准上实现 <1.5% 绝对精度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer 在训练后量化中因非线性层（LayerNorm 的 scale 参数与 GELU 的复合近似）产生结构性误差，导致需要浮点回退，难以实现全整数推理。

Method: TR-PTQ 提出一种统一的整数-only 量化公式，核心是共享 Taylor Region（TR）指数与对数原语。方法首先识别出 LayerNorm 中学习到的 scale 参数和 GELU 的复合近似是量化误差的主要来源，而 SoftMax 对量化鲁棒。为消除浮点运算，TR-PTQ 将除法、平方根等运算转换到 log-domain，通过标准整数算术完成。同时使用免校准、离群值感知的优化策略调整 LayerNorm 参数，以减小量化误差。整个推理路径不再需要浮点硬件单元处理非线性，从而支持全整数 Transformer 部署。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "绝对精度下降", "value": "<1.5%", "setting": "视觉与语言基准，整数-only Transformer PTQ"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 证明归一化层学习到的 scale 参数与 GELU 的复合近似是主要误差来源，而 SoftMax 对激进量化具有内在鲁棒性；提出基于共享 Taylor Region（TR）的指数与对数原语的统一整数-only 公式；通过 log-domain 整数算术实现除法、平方根等昂贵运算，消除非线性对浮点硬件的依赖；提出免校准、离群值感知的 LayerNorm 参数优化

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) enables efficient deployment, yet transformer architectures remain challenging to quantize due to nonlinear layers. While existing methods attribute accuracy loss to insufficient numerical precision, often necessitating floating-point fallbacks, we demonstrate that degradation is actually driven by specific structural error sources. We find that learned scale parameters in normalization layers and compounded approximations in GELU are the primary error contributors, whereas SoftMax remains inherently robust to aggressive quantization. To address these bottlenecks, we introduce TR-PTQ, a unified integer-only formulation using shared Taylor Region (TR) exponential and logarithm primitives. This approach allows computationally expensive operations, including division and square roots, to be performed entirely in the log-domain via standard integer arithmetic. Combined with a calibration-free, outlier-aware optimization for LayerNorm parameters, our method eliminates the need for floating-point hardware units for nonlinearities, achieving less than 1.5\% absolute accuracy degradation across vision and language benchmarks.

</details>

*Samy Houache,Yann Traonmilin,Jean-François Aujol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于层级概率误差分析的混合精度训练后量化比特分配方法，通过分离传播误差和局部扰动构建可分离分数，实现无需求解器的快速分配，并在校准数据损坏下具有鲁棒性。在DRUNet去噪任务上PSNR最高提升7.5 dB，比特分配加速28x-2570x。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合精度训练后量化中，在全局内存预算下进行逐层比特分配的组合优化问题，以及量化方法对校准数据损坏的鲁棒性问题。

Method: 论文提出一种基于层级概率误差分析的混合精度PTQ比特分配方法。首先，对量化误差进行层级概率分解，将误差分为从前面层传播而来的误差和当前层引入的局部扰动两部分。然后，利用局部扰动项构建每一层的可分离分数，该分数反映该层对量化误差的敏感度。在全局内存预算约束下，采用简单的贪心分配算法，根据分数逐层分配比特数，无需调用组合优化求解器。由于概率建模考虑了校准数据的分布，该方法对校准数据中的损坏（如噪声或异常值）具有鲁棒性。推理时，根据分配好的比特宽度对每层权重进行量化，并执行标准的前向计算。在DRUNet去噪任务和量化扩散模型上进行了实验验证。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "PSNR", "value": "最高提升7.5 dB", "setting": "在测试的校准数据损坏下，DRUNet去噪任务，平均每权重4比特预算"}, {"metric": "比特分配加速比", "value": "28x 到 2,570x", "setting": "与所研究的基线方法相比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 推导了量化误差的层级概率分析，将传播误差与局部扰动分离；利用局部误差项构建可分离的分配分数，实现无需外部求解器的快速比特分配算法；概率建模带来了对损坏校准数据的鲁棒性

Abstract: Mixed-precision post-training quantization is a network compression method that assigns bits layer by layer, under a global memory budget using a small calibration set. The main difficulties are to overcome the combinatorial nature of the allocation problem and to manage the sensitivity to small, potentially corrupted databases. Hence, an efficient allocation method should be fast to compute and preserve model quality when calibration data are corrupted. To design such a method, we derive a layerwise probabilistic analysis of the quantization error that separates propagated error from the local perturbation introduced at a given layer. We use this local term to build a separable score for a simple allocation algorithm, that requires no external solver. The probabilistic nature of our approach brings robustness to corrupted data. On denoising tasks with DRUNet, with an average budget of 4 bits per weight, our method matches or improves state-of-the-art mixed-precision baselines under clean calibration, and is more robust to corrupted calibration, with PSNR gains of up to 7.5 dB under the tested corruptions. Experiments show bit-allocation speed-ups from 28x to 2,570x over the studied baselines. For quantized diffusion models, our experiments show that a direct application of our framework also improves the state-of-the-art.

</details>


### [39] [NeuralZip: Reusable Setup for Fast Lossless Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09916)
*Martín Bravo,Samuel Horváth,Gonzalo Navarro,Andrés Abeliuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuralZip 提出一种可复用的无损压缩设置，通过分组共享 Huffman 码和打包指数表示浮点模型权重，在保持位级精确的前提下将后设置压缩速度提升 1.81-21.33 倍，并能在 GPU 上降低最高 27.5% 活动显存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在保持浮点权重位级精确的前提下，如何降低无损压缩的重复统计分析和编码构建开销，并快速压缩模型检查点。

Method: NeuralZip 面向浮点模型权重（如检查点）的无损压缩。输入为张量中的浮点数值，核心表示基于指数部分。系统首先通过一个 setup 阶段分析指数统计分布：将具有相似指数分布的块分组，为每组共享 Huffman 码；同时识别频繁出现的指数元组，将其表示为打包指数形式。setup 决定这些表示方式，可在后续编码前预计算，甚至从另一个兼容架构迁移，从而避免每次压缩都重新进行统计分析和码构建。实际编码阶段仍逐个处理当前张量的值，使用 setup 确定的共享 Huffman 码和打包指数规则进行熵编码，确保位级精确重建。训练检查点场景下，权重演化时设置可继续复用。GPU 实验中，该压缩表示也用于减少活动显存，同时能精确重建 logits。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "压缩速度", "value": "1.81-21.33× faster than baselines", "setting": "浮点模型检查点，post-setup 压缩阶段"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将指数分布相似的块分组并共享 Huffman 码，减少码本构建开销；对重复出现的指数元组使用打包指数表示，获得额外压缩率；设置（setup）可预计算并从兼容架构迁移，避免为每次编码摊薄设置成本；支持训练检查点随权重演化继续复用设置；在 GPU 上实现减少活动显存占用并精确复现 logits

Abstract: Lossless compression can reduce the storage and movement of model weights without changing their floating-point values, but repeated statistical analysis and code construction add computational overhead. We study whether the statistical structure of exponents can be prepared once and reused. For this, we introduce NeuralZip, which groups chunks with similar exponent distributions, shares Huffman codes, and selectively represents recurring exponent tuples using packed exponents, thereby achieving additional moderate compression ratios. A setup chooses these representations before subsequent encodings, while every encoding still processes the current tensor values. In floating-point model checkpoints, post-setup compression is 1.81-21.33$\times$ faster than the baselines and achieves exact bit-to-bit reconstruction. We show that this setup can be precomputed and transferred from another compatible architecture, preserving similar compression ratios and avoiding the need to amortize setup costs. Therefore, compression adaptation is transferable and reusable. Training checkpoints demonstrate continued reuse as the weights evolve. Finally, GPU experiments reduce active memory usage by up to 27.5$\%$ while reproducing the logits exactly.

</details>


### [40] [BoT-GRPO: Efficient Process-Reward RL for Reasoning via Bag-of-Token Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09804)
*Yingxiang Yang,Weihang Xiao,Zhunxuan Wang,Joshua Flashner,Niresh Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BoT-GRPO扩展GRPO至token级奖励，通过长度不变的bag-of-tokens加权聚合计算优势，无需critic。在React代码生成和AIME数学推理上，收敛更快、最终质量更高，且发现奖励稳定性比丰富性更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GRPO等策略优化中，rollout内的每个token获得相同优势，如何在不引入价值网络的情况下实现高效的过程监督，加速收敛并提升最终质量。

Method: BoT-GRPO在GRPO框架基础上引入token级奖励模型。训练时，对每个问题采样一组rollout序列，收集所有token级奖励，按每个token所在源序列长度的倒数进行加权，然后基于加权后的组统计量计算每个token的优势值。该优势用于策略梯度更新，无需训练critic价值网络。推理时，模型使用标准自回归生成，无需额外组件。该方法直接替换GRPO中的优势计算，适用于任何有token级奖励的场景。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "编译率", "value": "80%", "setting": "React前端代码生成任务，达到80%编译率的速度比GRPO快1.9倍"}, {"metric": "收敛速度与最终质量", "value": "收敛更快（未量化），最终编译率和VLM评判胜率更高", "setting": "React前端代码生成任务，与GSPO、DAPO、PURE等现代GRPO变体对比"}, {"metric": "Pass@k绝对提升", "value": "最高8.1%", "setting": "AIME数学推理任务，相比GRPO，步数减半"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出BoT-GRPO，通过长度不变的“bag-of-tokens”聚合将GRPO扩展到token级奖励模型；收集所有rollout的token级奖励，按源序列长度的倒数加权，并基于加权组统计计算每个token的优势；无critic设计，可直接替代GRPO（当有token级奖励时）；实验得出奖励模型设计经验：奖励稳定性比丰富性更重要，干净、有界、稳定的细粒度信号能持续加速学习

Abstract: Reinforcement learning is now central to eliciting reasoning in large language models, while in the popular algorithm Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) every token in a rollout receives the same advantage. We ask how to make process supervision efficient: accelerating convergence and improving final quality without the cost of value networks. We propose Bag-of-Tokens Group Relative Policy Optimization (BoT-GRPO), which extends GRPO to token-level reward models through a length-invariant "bag of tokens" aggregation: it collects all token-level rewards across rollouts, weights each by the inverse of its source sequence length, and computes per-token advantages relative to weighted group statistics. BoT-GRPO is critic-free, and is a drop-in replacement wherever GRPO is used when token-level reward is available. On React front-end code generation, BoT-GRPO reaches $80\%$ compile rate up to $1.9\times$ faster than GRPO and converges faster than modern GRPO variants (GSPO, DAPO, PURE) while reaching higher final compile and VLM-judged win rates. On a second task, AIME mathematical reasoning, BoT-GRPO delivers absolute Pass@$k$ gains up to $8.1\%$ over GRPO in half the steps. For both tasks we compare the algorithm's performance on reasoning vs. non-reasoning base-model families (Qwen2.5-3B, SmolLM3-3B, Phi-4-mini-reasoning). Our experiments also yield a practical recipe for the reward model itself: reward stability matters more than richness: clean, bounded, stable fine-grained signals consistently accelerate learning where noisier alternatives stall.

</details>


### [41] [Dual-QK: Sharp Queries and Flat Keys for Prunable 2-bit KV Caches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09827)
*Sunjoo Whang,Jungjun Oh,Minsung Kim,Dongho Seo,Jisu Shin,Gregory Kielian,Hoi-Jun Yoo,Sangjin Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对长上下文 LLM 推理中 KV 缓存存储与访问开销大的问题，Dual-QK 提出成对的非正交查询/键变换，结合部分键白化与查询对齐基，在 2-bit 键量化和查询通道剪枝间取得平衡，并引入通道 0 保护和 bucket-relative RoPE 维持长上下文精度。在四个模型、五个生成基准和长上下文检索上优于 OSCAR，128K 上下文下实现 6.8 倍 KV 缓存压缩、8.3 倍 KV 读取量减少，SGLang 实现解码吞吐最高提升 3.75 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转式低比特量化要求对查询和键应用相同的正交变换以保持点积不变性，但这会分散查询能量，削弱少量大分量与大量小分量之间的区分度，从而降低查询通道剪枝的有效性；如何在保持低比特量化精度的同时实现有效的通道剪枝。

Method: Dual-QK 是一种针对 KV 缓存的低比特量化与查询通道剪枝联合优化方法。输入为长上下文序列，模型生成过程中键和查询分别经过成对的非正交变换：键端应用基于校准统计的部分键白化，将键的能量重新分布以平衡各通道尺度，从而适配 INT2 量化；查询端应用查询对齐基，将查询能量集中到少数通道，使得按通道动态剪枝可以在保留关键分量的同时丢弃低贡献通道。为维持长上下文精度，引入通道 0 保护（保留该通道全精度）和 bucket-relative RoPE（对旋转位置编码进行分桶处理，缓解长距离位置编码对低比特量化的敏感度）。训练目标为最小化量化与剪枝带来的精度损失，通过校准数据集估计查询和键的统计量来优化变换矩阵。推理时，KV 缓存以 2-bit 存储，查询通道按稀疏度要求动态剪枝，键缓存读取量大幅减少；在 SGLang 框架中实现，支持 128K 上下文，实现 6.8 倍缓存压缩和最高 3.75 倍解码吞吐提升。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率", "value": "在大多数任务上优于 OSCAR（40% 查询通道稀疏度）", "setting": "四个模型、五个生成基准和长上下文检索任务"}, {"metric": "KV 缓存压缩率", "value": "6.8 倍", "setting": "128K 上下文"}, {"metric": "KV 读取量减少", "value": "8.3 倍", "setting": "128K 上下文，相对于未剪枝 BF16"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 成对的非正交查询与键变换，打破正交约束以同时优化量化与剪枝；基于校准统计的部分键白化（partial key whitening）平衡 INT2 量化下的键尺度；查询对齐基（query-aligned basis）集中查询能量以支持动态通道剪枝；通道 0 保护（Channel-0 protection）与 bucket-relative RoPE 提升长上下文低比特精度

Abstract: Long inputs and extended generation increase the storage and access costs of the key-value (KV) cache. Low-bit quantization reduces storage and memory traffic, while query-channel pruning can further reduce key-cache reads. Rotation-based quantization redistributes the energy of key outliers across channels. To maintain computational invariance, the same orthogonal transform must be applied to queries, preserving query-key dot products. However, this rotation can disperse query energy, weakening the separation between a few large components to retain and many small ones to prune. We introduce Dual-QK, which uses paired non-orthogonal query and key transforms to address this conflict. Using calibrated query and key statistics, Dual-QK combines partial key whitening with a query-aligned basis to balance key scales for INT2 quantization and concentrate query energy for dynamic channel pruning. Channel-0 protection and bucket-relative RoPE support low-bit accuracy over long contexts. Experiments on four models across five generative benchmarks and long-context retrieval tasks show improved accuracy over OSCAR on most tasks at 40% query-channel sparsity. At a 128K context, Dual-QK provides $6.8\times$ KV-cache compression and an estimated $8.3\times$ reduction in KV read volume relative to unpruned BF16. Under the evaluated configurations, our SGLang implementation achieves up to $3.75\times$ the decoding throughput of unpruned BF16.

</details>

Method: EntroPrefill在prefill阶段中期执行上下文剪枝，以减少更深层transformer需要处理的序列长度。流程为：(1) 用Renyi引导的提议机制对token重要性进行打分，同时显式约束被丢弃token所携带的注意力质量上限；(2) 采用sink隔离（隔离attention sink）且正则化的注意力头池化，在兼容分组查询注意力(GQA)的前提下聚合多头信号，并刻画专门化头与最差head覆盖之间的定量权衡；(3) 基于混合到头的删除包络推导可行token删除量的可计算上界，并给出剪枝层可自适应选择时仍成立的有限样本观测保证；(4) 建立条件transformer扰动界，给出显式充分Lipschitz常数及首token决策边界推论，并用反例表明浅层观测无法推出无条件的未来输出保证；(5) 系统层面区分query-head并集、物理页分配与KV传输负载，并给出剪枝的算术盈亏平衡条件。论文为纯理论工作，未报告实测加速或任务准确率保持，实验留待后续验证。

Conclusion: 用Renyi引导的提议机制结合对丢弃注意力质量的显式约束来指导剪枝；sink隔离且正则化的head pooling，兼容GQA并刻画专门化与最差head覆盖的权衡；推导mixture-to-head删除包络、可行token删除量的可计算上界，以及剪枝层自适应选择下仍有效的有限样本观测保证；建立带显式充分Lipschitz常数的条件transformer扰动界与首token决策边界推论；给出反例说明浅层观测不能推出无条件的未来输出保证；系统分析区分query-head并集、物理页分配与KV传输负载，并给出剪枝的算术盈亏平衡条件

Abstract: Mid-prefill pruning can reduce the sequence processed by deeper transformer layers, but attention concentration alone does not certify that discarded context is dispensable. We formulate EntroPrefill as a Renyi-guided proposal mechanism coupled to explicit constraints on discarded attention mass. Sink-isolated, regularized head pooling respects grouped-query attention while exposing a quantitative trade-off between specialization and worst-head coverage. We derive a mixture-to-head deletion envelope, a computable upper bound on feasible token removal, and a finite-sample observer guarantee that remains valid when the pruning layer is selected adaptively. We then establish a conditional transformer perturbation bound with explicit sufficient Lipschitz constants and a first-token decision-margin corollary. A counterexample shows why shallow observations alone cannot imply an unconditional future-output guarantee. The systems analysis distinguishes query-head unions, physical page allocation, and KV-transfer payload, and gives an arithmetic break-even condition for pruning. This manuscript is theoretical in scope: it defines the procedure, its assumptions, and its formal limits, but does not report measured acceleration or task-accuracy preservation. Experiments are reserved for subsequent validation of the assumptions, approximation tightness, and end-to-end resource trade-offs.

</details>

*Zhaohui Geoffrey Wang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM后训练中，从业者常假设表示秩下降即表示退化，但该单向假设在非平稳环境下是否可靠、谱监控能否比held-out loss更早预警，尚未被系统检验。

Method: 研究对象为Qwen3-0.6B的LLM后训练流程。输入为在非平稳环境下进行后训练时的模型中间层表示状态。监控模块计算两类谱统计量：RankMe对表示矩阵的奇异值做归一化，协方差有效秩对协方差矩阵的特征值做归一化；论文进一步在预训练模型原始中间层状态上分析二者差异，发现massive activations使协方差有效秩被钉在约1/d，而RankMe仍保留可用范围。为应对单向监控失效，作者构建双侧、多通道序贯监控器：将RankMe、中心化RankMe、k95等统计量作为多通道信号，使用独立校准数据与测试数据进行校准，然后对后训练过程中的每个checkpoint进行序贯判定。训练目标方面不对模型本身优化做改动，而是以监控统计量的方向（上升或下降）联合判定损伤机制：谱分散型损伤表现为秩上升，降秩型损伤（如学习率配置错误）表现为中心化RankMe与k95下降。推理/服务路径即在线训练循环中按checkpoint流式计算谱统计量并触发告警。评估采用预注册的shared-prefix、leave-one-seed-out协议，检验检测时延、损伤类型区分能力及校准数据不足（仅两个种子）时对held-out健康种子的误报情况。

Conclusion: 揭示了数据重复导致held-out loss恶化75%的同时RankMe与中心化RankMe反而上升的谱分散（spectral dispersion）失效模式，说明单向监控会把最差checkpoint评为最健康；区分RankMe（归一化奇异值）与协方差有效秩（归一化特征值）两种常被混淆的统计量，并指出预训练模型中间层原始状态中massive activations使后者被钉在接近1/d；提出并预注册评估双侧多通道序贯监控器，分离校准与测试数据，可在分叉后10-60步内检测三种损伤机制并按方向区分分散型与降秩型损伤

Abstract: Post-training adapts language models in non-stationary environments. Practitioners monitor representation health with RankMe and related spectral statistics, often assuming that rank falls when representations degrade. We show that this assumption is unsafe for LLM post-training. In a controlled study of Qwen3-0.6B with four degradation modes and three seeds, data duplication worsens held-out loss by 75% relative to healthy while increasing both original and centred RankMe; the latter changes by 13.5 pooled standard deviations. Covariance effective rank rises to nearly twice its healthy value. This failure is spectral dispersion rather than collapse, so a one-sided monitor rates the worst checkpoint as the healthiest. By contrast, a learning-rate misconfiguration lowers centred RankMe and k95, while uncentred RankMe is inconsistent across seeds. Direction is therefore a property of the regime-statistic pair and cannot be fixed by recalibration alone. We also distinguish two often-conflated statistics: RankMe normalises singular values, whereas covariance effective rank normalises eigenvalues. On raw intermediate-layer states in the pretrained model, massive activations pin the latter near 1 out of dimension d while RankMe retains usable range. We then test a two-sided, multichannel sequential monitor with separate calibration and test data. In a pre-registered shared-prefix, leave-one-seed-out evaluation, it detects all three damage regimes in every fold 10 to 60 steps after the fork and separates dispersion from downward-rank damage by firing direction. However, it never precedes held-out probe loss, and calibration with two seeds produces false alarms on the held-out healthy seed. Spectral monitoring can diagnose failure regimes, but it does not warn earlier than held-out loss, and validity claims require held-out healthy data.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何利用训练网络的几何与谱结构（谱集中、物理通道对齐、支撑结构、奇异基变化）来指导模型修改、重配置和压缩中的关键设计选择。

Method: CHASE的核心是几何与谱对齐（GSA），它通过谱集中度、物理通道对齐、支撑结构和奇异基变化来刻画训练后的网络。该方法基于这些结构设计具体操作：CAGA首先利用GSA识别适合共享KV表示的多头注意力头，然后通过几何对齐和低秩子空间提取来构造共享的键和值头，从而将MHA转换为GQA；SAKV使用GSA确定哪些相邻层可以共享低秩KV-cache表示，并为每个层组确定保留的秩；CAPS利用GSA的谱结构对输出神经元进行分组，并为每组单独选择保留的输入通道。此外，CORA、COEC和CORAM分别将GSA应用于参数高效微调、结构化剪枝补偿和模型合并。整体架构覆盖模型修改、重配置和压缩三个层面，但论文摘要未披露具体的输入表示、处理顺序、训练目标和推理流程细节，因此无法提供更详细的端到端架构描述。

Conclusion: 提出统一框架CHASE，将几何与谱对齐（GSA）应用于六类模型操作。；CAGA：利用GSA识别可共享KV表示的多头注意力头，通过几何对齐和低秩子空间提取构建共享键值头，改进MHA到GQA的转换。；SAKV：利用GSA确定相邻层可共享低秩KV-cache表示及每组保留的秩。；CAPS：利用GSA谱结构对输出神经元分组并保留每组输入通道进行结构化剪枝。

Abstract: Geometric and Spectral Alignment (GSA) characterizes trained networks through spectral concentration, physical-channel alignment, support structure, and changes in singular bases. In this paper, we propose CHASE (Channel-Aligned Structure Exploitation) to use these structures in practical model design. CHASE covers six applications across model modification, reconfiguration, and compression. CORA, COEC, and CORAM apply GSA to parameter-efficient finetuning, structured-pruning compensation, and model merging. We further develop three new methods. CAGA uses GSA to identify multi-head attention heads that can share a KV representation and constructs the shared key and value heads through geometric alignment and low-rank subspace extraction. SAKV uses GSA to determine which adjacent layers can share a low-rank KV-cache representation and the retained rank for each layer group. CAPS uses GSA spectral structure to group output neurons and selects retained input channels separately for each group. Results from CORA, COEC, and CORAM establish the effectiveness of GSA for adaptation, pruning compensation, and model merging. Experiments on CAGA show that geometric shared-head construction substantially improves MHA-to-GQA conversion, and SAKV and CAPS improve over representative baselines for KV-cache compression and structured pruning. These results show that the structures identified by GSA can be used directly to design methods for a range of model operations.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两级softmax（2LS）采样因忽略簇大小不均衡和簇内相似度离散度而引入系统性偏差，导致近似不准确。

Method: 两级softmax（2LS）采样假设物品被划分为多个簇。采样过程分两步：首先根据簇的权重采样一个簇，然后在选中的簇内采样一个物品。标准2LS忽略了簇大小的不均衡和簇内相似度的离散度，导致采样概率偏离真实softmax分布。论文提出S-2LS和SD-2LS方法，通过修正簇的采样权重来校正这些偏差。S-2LS仅修正簇大小不均衡，SD-2LS进一步修正簇内离散度。修正后的采样在保持亚线性时间复杂度的同时，提供了可证明更优的softmax近似。实验在五个大规模数据集上验证了改进的采样性质。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 揭示了两级softmax采样中由簇大小不均衡和簇内离散度导致的偏差。；提出Size-Corrected 2LS（S-2LS）修正簇大小不均衡偏差。；提出Size- and Dispersion-Corrected 2LS（SD-2LS）同时修正大小和离散度偏差。；理论证明修正后采样能更好地逼近softmax分布，且计算开销可忽略。

Abstract: Sampling from a softmax distribution is a fundamental operation in machine learning, but its linear complexity in the number of items makes exact sampling impractical at scale. Two-level softmax (2LS) sampling is a popular alternative enabling sublinear-time sampling. Assuming items are partitioned into clusters, 2LS first samples a cluster and then an item within it. In this paper, we show that, despite its advantages, 2LS introduces systematic and undesirable sampling biases, which arise from misweighting clusters by ignoring both cluster size imbalance and intra-cluster similarity dispersion. We propose two sampling methods, Size-Corrected 2LS (S-2LS) and Size- and Dispersion-Corrected 2LS (SD-2LS), which correct these biases and provide provably better softmax approximations with negligible to non-existent computational overhead. In-depth experiments on five large-scale datasets validate the improved sampling properties of our methods. We recommend their consistent use in place of standard 2LS in future work.

</details>


### [46] [Denoising Blocks, Not Tokens: Efficient Compressed Continuous Diffusion with Branching Token Realization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09311)
*Xinsong Feng,Peng Du,Zhizhuo Yang,Daniel M. Bikel,Jiayun Wang,Haipeng Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BLD 将 1024 token 压缩为 64 个连续 block latent，在潜在序列上做扩散，并用并行局部 AR 分支解码，相同 GPU 下端到端生成 FLOPs 降低超过 80 倍、吞吐量相比相近规模 ELF-L 提升超过 6 倍，相比 AR 基线吞吐量提升超过 6 倍、延迟降低超过 4 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型(DLM)通常为每个 token 维护一个生成状态，导致每个去噪步骤要处理与输出序列等长的状态序列，限制了并行生成的吞吐量提升。

Method: BLD 面向连续扩散语言模型，将原本按 token 维护的生成状态改为在压缩的连续潜在空间上工作。输入文本序列首先被压缩为 block latent 表示，论文中把 1024 个 token 压缩为 64 个 block latent，压缩比为 16 倍。扩散过程在这些较短的基础 latent 序列上进行，而非等长的 token 状态序列，从而减少每个去噪步骤需要处理的状态长度。由于强压缩使得一次性联合生成所有 latent 较困难，BLD 将 latent 分组生成，每组条件于此前已生成的 latent，逐步构建完整的 latent 序列。得到 latent 序列后，每个 latent 由一个局部自回归分支解码为对应的 token 块，所有分支并行执行，这一过程称为 branching token realization，即将压缩后的连续状态扩展为离散 token 输出。整体目标是结合连续扩散的灵活性与局部自回归解码的并行性，在保持生成局部流畅性和多样性的同时提升长序列生成的效率。评估在相同 GPU 上进行，测量生成 FLOPs、吞吐量和延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "generation FLOPs", "value": "降低超过 80 倍", "setting": "在相同 GPU 上进行端到端评估，与规模相近的 ELF-L 基线相比"}, {"metric": "throughput", "value": "提升超过 6 倍", "setting": "在相同 GPU 上进行端到端评估，与规模相近的 ELF-L 基线相比"}, {"metric": "throughput", "value": "提升超过 6 倍", "setting": "与 AR 基线相比"}, {"metric": "latency", "value": "降低超过 4 倍", "setting": "与 AR 基线相比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Branching Latent Diffusion (BLD)，将 1024 个 token 的序列压缩为 64 个 block latent，实现 16 倍压缩。；结合 branching token realization，每个 latent 由一个局部自回归分支解码，所有分支并行执行。；由于强压缩使联合 latent 生成困难，BLD 按组生成 latent，每一组以前面已生成的 latent 为条件。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) generate text through iterative parallel refinement, offering the potential for higher throughput than autoregressive (AR) decoding. However, most DLMs still maintain one generative state per token, so every denoising step processes a state sequence as long as the output sequence, limiting the throughput gains from parallel generation. Continuous DLMs provide an additional degree of freedom: a single continuous state can represent multiple tokens, allowing diffusion to operate on a much shorter latent sequence. We introduce \emph{Branching Latent Diffusion (BLD)}, which exploits this flexibility by compressing a 1024-token sequence into only 64 block latents, a $16\times$ reduction. BLD combines latent compression with \emph{branching token realization}, where each latent is decoded by a local AR branch and all branches run in parallel. Because strong compression makes joint latent generation difficult, BLD generates the latents in groups, conditioning each group on previously generated latents. In end-to-end evaluation on the same GPU, BLD reduces generation FLOPs by more than $80\times$ and increases throughput by more than $6\times$ relative to the similarly sized ELF-L baseline. Compared with the AR baseline, BLD achieves more than $6\times$ higher throughput and more than $4\times$ lower latency. Despite the compression, BLD maintains competitive local fluency and diversity, although long-range coherence remains challenging. Overall, BLD shows that moving diffusion from token-level states to compressed latent sequences can substantially improve the efficiency of long-sequence generation.

</details>


### [47] [Conditional Accuracy Profiles: Diagnosing LLM Judges across Deployment Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09229)
*Wenqi Li,Bin Liu,Mindi Ruan,Chuanbo Hu,Minglei Yin,Xin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出 CAP 框架，将 LLM-as-judge 的成对准确率分解为内容敏感性、鲁棒性和理由质量共八个条件，在七个 LLM 评审与六个基准上验证，揭示总体准确率无法预测条件级表现，为部署条件下选择 LLM 评审提供更可操作的依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-judge 的性能通常被单一总体准确率掩盖，无法反映其在具体部署条件（内容敏感性、鲁棒性、理由质量）下的失败模式，导致难以可靠地选择与部署 LLM 评审模型。

Method: CAP 是一个事后诊断框架，不改变评审模型本身。输入为成对评判基准数据，包含待评判的成对样本、LLM 评审模型给出的判断以及所需的标注或扰动词料。CAP 将成对评审准确率按条件分解为八个条件，归入三类：内容敏感性（如遗漏限定条件、事实性等）、鲁棒性（如位置鲁棒性、对抗扰动等）、理由质量。实例化时支持三种模式：若基准已提供所需标注则直接按条件切片计算；若标注不全，则通过任务子集代理近似条件；若可生成扰动对，则通过受控增强构造条件。评估输出为每个 LLM 评审在各条件下的准确率画像及跨基准排名稳定性。论文在七个 LLM 评审和六个成对评判基准上实例化，并构建 JudgerEva-Standard 受控测试平台以覆盖全部八个条件，同时使用 JudgeBench-Pro 进行对抗压力测试，观察各评审在不同退化通道下的表现差异。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "Position Robustness 跨基准排名稳定性", "value": "mean Spearman ρ=0.87", "setting": "七个 LLM 评审、六个成对评审基准"}, {"metric": "遗漏限定条件敏感性与总体准确率排名错位", "value": "两个对遗漏限定条件最敏感的评审模型在七者中总体准确率排名后三", "setting": "JudgerEva 的 judge-independent Hard-Constructed 子集"}, {"metric": "JudgeBench-Pro 对抗压力下 Position Robustness 平均准确率下降最大", "value": "shared conditions 中最大平均准确率降幅", "setting": "JudgeBench-Pro 对抗压力测试，七个 LLM 评审"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出 Conditional Accuracy Profiling (CAP)，将成对 LLM 评审准确率事后分解为八个可解释条件，覆盖内容敏感性、鲁棒性和理由质量三类。；设计 benchmark-agnostic 的实例化路径：直接标注、任务子集代理、扰动对增强三种方式，使 CAP 适配不同基准。；构建 JudgerEva-Standard 受控测试平台，支持全部八个条件的稳定评测，并引入 JudgeBench-Pro 等对抗压力测试。；揭示总体准确率无法预测条件级表现：在 Hard-Constructed 子集上，对遗漏限定条件最敏感的两个评审模型整体准确率排名却处于七者中的后三名。；发现 Position Robustness 在跨基准上排名稳定性最强（平均 Spearman ρ=0.87），但在 JudgeBench-Pro 对抗压力下退化最大，且主导退化通道因评审模型而异。

Abstract: LLM-as-judge is now a standard tool for scalable evaluation, but judge performance is still often summarized by a single accuracy number. This aggregate view hides the deployment conditions under which a judge succeeds or fails. We introduce \textbf{Conditional Accuracy Profiling} (CAP), a post-hoc diagnostic framework that decomposes pairwise LLM-judge accuracy into eight conditions organized into content sensitivity, robustness, and rationale quality. CAP is benchmark-agnostic: it can be applied directly when a benchmark provides the required annotations, approximately through task-subset proxies, or through controlled augmentation when perturbation pairs can be generated. We instantiate CAP on seven LLM judges across six pairwise judging benchmarks, including \textsc{judgerEva-Standard}, a controlled testbed we created to support all eight conditions. CAP exposes profile differences hidden by aggregate accuracy: on \textsc{judgerEva}'s judge-independent Hard-Constructed subset, the two judges most sensitive to omitted qualifications rank in the bottom three of seven by overall accuracy, so omission sensitivity is not predicted by aggregate accuracy. Across benchmarks, Position Robustness shows the strongest rank stability (mean Spearman $\bar{\rho}{=}0.87$) but is itself fragile under JudgeBench-Pro adversarial stress, showing the largest mean accuracy drop among the shared conditions, though the dominant degradation channel varies by judge. Condition-level profiles provide a more actionable basis than aggregate accuracy for selecting LLM judges.

</details>


### [48] [CurveTQ: Rotation-Free Trellis Quantization of LLM Weights via Curvature-Weighted Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09212)
*Guanhua Ding,Zi Wang,Ruichao Li,Jack Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CurveTQ 提出一种无需旋转的2比特网格量化方法，将海森矩阵曲率权重放入 Viterbi 搜索，在多个模型上超越 QTIP 和 Proteus，并实现更快解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最优2比特权重量化器（QTIP、Proteus）通过随机正交旋转并需要每步解码还原旋转，且仅通过误差反馈利用海森矩阵，未充分利用海森曲率信息；同时旋转带来额外解码开销。

Method: CurveTQ 是一个无旋转的网格量化编解码器。输入为预训练大语言模型的权重矩阵。编码时，首先对每个权重矩阵计算海森矩阵的 LDL 分解，取对角元素作为每个坐标的曲率权重。然后使用因子化尺度场处理权重的幅度分布，并利用闭式分位表匹配权重的边缘形状。接着，将曲率权重放入 Viterbi 分支度量，在网格编码块内进行加权搜索，寻找最优量化路径。同时，为每个编码块存储一个起始状态，该状态由误差反馈携带的残差决定，使网格能够自适应残差。解码时，不需要任何随机正交旋转的还原，直接根据存储的起始状态和网格路径重建权重，因此解码速度最快。整个流程不涉及旋转操作，减少了计算开销。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均下游准确率", "value": "比 QTIP 和 Proteus 高 1-3 个点", "setting": "2比特，三个 4-8B Instruct 模型"}, {"metric": "平均下游准确率提升", "value": "1-3 个点", "setting": "给予基线相同的起始状态后"}, {"metric": "下游准确率差距", "value": "约 1 个点以内", "setting": "加权原生搜索与全维随机 Hadamard 旋转对比，三个模型"}, {"metric": "解码速度", "value": "最快", "setting": "所有测试批次大小和比特宽度，与 QTIP、Proteus 对比"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将海森矩阵 LDL 分解的对角权重直接放入 Viterbi 分支度量，使搜索在编码块内跟随曲率，无需旋转。；论证旋转与在原生基中加权是替代关系，旋转消除了块内曲率变化。；提出 CurveTQ 无旋转网格编解码器：因子化尺度场处理权重幅度与边缘形状，闭式分位表，并为每个编码块存储起始状态以适配误差反馈带来的残差。；给出首个在 35B 混合专家模型上的网格编码量化结果。

Abstract: The best two-bit weight quantizers for large language models, such as QTIP and Proteus, rotate each weight matrix by a random orthogonal transform, which must be undone at every decoding step, then encode it with a trellis or lattice code under a Euclidean search; the layer Hessian enters only through error feedback between coding blocks. We show that this leaves part of the Hessian unused. Error feedback turns the loss into a weighted sum of per-coordinate rounding errors whose weights, the diagonal of the Hessian's LDL factorization, existing quantizers compute but never read. We put these weights into the Viterbi branch metric, so the search follows the curvature within each coding block. This also explains the rotation: it removes this within-block variation, so weighting in the native basis and rotating are substitutes. On three models the weighted native search matches a full-dimension randomized Hadamard to within about one point of downstream accuracy, and weighting after the rotation gains little. Around this search we build CurveTQ, a trellis codec with no rotation, which handles the weights' amplitude and marginal shape with a factored scale field and a closed-form quantile table, and stores a start state per coding block so the trellis can adapt to the residual that error feedback carries into it. At two bits CurveTQ is 1-3 points higher in mean downstream accuracy than QTIP and Proteus on three 4-8B Instruct models, even after both are given our start state, which alone lifts either baseline by 1-3 points. It also leads on a 35B mixture of experts, to our knowledge the first trellis-coded result on such a model. With no rotation to undo, our decoder is the fastest of the three at all tested batch sizes and bit widths.

</details>


### [49] [The Dichotomy Between Pattern Recognition and Step-by-Step Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09186)
*Amrut Nadgir,Pratik Chaudhari,Vijay Balasubramanian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从理论和实验角度探讨LLM逐步推理与模式识别的 dichotomy，提出基于De Bruijn图的推理结构，并证明真实数据具有该结构，为LLM推理的样本效率和注意力窗口优化提供见解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在何种数据结构下学习逐步推理，以及推理痕迹的图结构如何影响样本效率、准确性和鲁棒性。

Method: 论文未提出新的端到端架构，而是基于标准Transformer架构。输入为序列化文本（方程或故事问题），模型通过自回归方式生成推理步骤。核心模块是注意力机制，实验通过限制注意力窗口大小来模拟De Bruijn结构中的上下文长度c。训练目标为标准的下一token预测。推理时，模型逐token生成，若数据具有De Bruijn结构，则每一步仅依赖于少量前文状态，从而允许使用滑动窗口注意力加速推理。论文通过微调Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct来验证不同c值下的准确率与鲁棒性权衡，但未披露微调的具体数据量、硬件或耗时。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "准确率保持率", "value": ">75%", "setting": "GSM8K, MATH-500, GPQA-Diamond，滑动窗口注意力<15%全推理长度，模型Qwen3-14B和Qwen3-32B"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将推理痕迹形式化为De Bruijn图的边，并证明边数远少于推理痕迹数，从而解释逐步推理的样本高效性。；提出通过维护'状态'来诱导任务的De Bruijn结构，状态频率决定上下文窗口大小c。；通过微调Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct实验展示频繁状态（小c）带来高准确率但对扰动脆弱，大c则退化为模式识别。；证明真实数据（GSM8K、MATH-500、GPQA-Diamond）具有De Bruijn结构，Qwen3-14B/32B在仅使用<15%注意力窗口时保持>75%准确率。

Abstract: We argue that pattern recognition and step-by-step reasoning are two ends of a spectrum. A large language model (LLM) learns to reason step-by-step when data is structured such that the next token depends on a small amount of preceding context. Inference in LLMs resembles pattern recognition when the next token depends on a large amount of preceding context. If the next token depends on only the $c$ most recent tokens, reasoning traces are paths on a De Bruijn graph whose nodes are $c$-length contexts and edges are next-token transitions between contexts. The set of reasoning traces of a task forms a directed acyclic subgraph of the De Bruijn graph. An LLM that has learned all edges of this subgraph can compose them to solve longer, unseen tasks, i.e., it reasons step-by-step. We prove that the number of edges is vanishingly small compared to the number of reasoning traces. Empirically, the number of training samples a transformer needs is a power law in the number of edges, so learning to reason step-by-step is sample efficient. We can induce De Bruijn structure in any task by maintaining a ``state'' that makes future reasoning independent of the past. The frequency of states in the reasoning trace determines $c$. We show, by fine-tuning Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct to solve equations and answer questions about stories, that frequent states (small $c$) result in higher accuracy but greater fragility to perturbations at test time. LLMs trained with a large $c$ are only as good as models that perform pattern recognition without reasoning. A moderate density of states balances accuracy and robustness. We show that real-world data has De Bruijn structure: Qwen3-14B and Qwen3-32B retain over 75% of their accuracy on GSM8K, MATH-500 and GPQA-Diamond when attention is restricted to a sliding window less than 15% as long as the full reasoning trace.

</details>


### [50] [Q-PACE: Dynamic Precision Allocation for Quantization-Aware Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09183)
*Alexandra Volkova,Matin Ansaripour,Erik Schultheis,Christoph H. Lampert,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Q-PACE 利用二阶敏感度模型动态分配混合精度，在 LLM 量化感知训练中实现更低内存开销下与现有方法相当的损失，敏感度可由深度和层类型预测且重校准成本低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量化感知训练中，如何动态地为不同层分配混合精度，以在降低训练和部署内存成本的同时保持模型性能。

Method: Q-PACE 是一种量化感知训练中的动态精度分配方法。其核心包含两个模块：敏感度预测器和精度分配调度器。敏感度预测器基于二阶泰勒展开近似，将量化引起的损失增量建模为各层量化噪声的均方误差（MSE）之和，每层噪声由该层的曲率系数加权。训练过程中，周期性地对各层施加扰动并测量损失变化，从而在线估计每层的曲率系数。得到系数后，调度器根据目标内存预算或精度约束，将高精度分配给曲率最大的层，低精度分配给不敏感的层，并重新配置各层的量化位宽。模型继续训练若干步，直至下一次重校准周期。该方法由于敏感度在训练过程中相对稳定，可采用较低的校准频率以控制额外开销。整体流程为：初始化量化模型 -> 周期性扰动估计曲率 -> 精度重分配 -> 继续训练 -> 重复。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "训练损失", "value": "与现有混合精度训练方法相当", "setting": "LLM最高4B参数，预训练和有监督微调，更低的总内存预算"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出二阶敏感度模型，将损失增量近似为各层量化噪声MSE的加权和，权重由逐层曲率系数决定。；在训练过程中周期性利用逐层扰动重新计算曲率系数，并据此重新分配精度。；发现量化敏感度可由模型深度和层类型高度预测，且具有稳定性，从而支持低频、低成本的重校准。

Abstract: Quantization-aware training (QAT) leverages lower-precision arithmetic to reduce the cost of LLM deployment, but aggressive quantization degrades final model performance. A common remedy is mixed-precision training, in which high precision is assigned to some of the layers to maintain performance while keeping the cost constrained. This approach then requires precision assignments for model layers during training. We provide a new approach, called Q-PACE, consisting of a second-order sensitivity model that predicts the loss increase as a sum of quantization noise MSE weighted by per-layer curvature coefficients. During training, we periodically re-compute these coefficients using perturbations across layers, and re-assign precision. Pretraining and supervised fine-tuning experiments on LLMs of up to 4B parameters show that Q-PACE consistently improves over existing mixed-precision training recipes, and achieves comparable loss at substantially lower total memory budgets. We further find that quantization sensitivity is highly predictable by depth and layer type, and its stability during training allows for infrequent, cheap recalibration.

</details>


### [51] [TAP: Efficient Long-Horizon Agent Pruning via Trajectory-Anchored Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09074)
*Yuanzhe Li,Pengxin Wang,Yuxin Ren,Jianing Deng,Jingtong Hu,Song Wang,Jingdi Chen,Huanrui Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TAP是首个面向RL训练长时程智能体的结构化剪枝框架，通过轨迹锚定在线恢复与基于恢复目标梯度的迭代通道重评分，在剪除60% FFN通道下保留ALFWorld 99.2%、WebShop 88.0%的任务成功率，并降低每成功任务GPU时间约22%和17%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向问答任务提出的剪枝方法直接迁移到长时程RL训练智能体时会严重退化任务性能，根因在于‘剪什么’（一次性重要性估计无法跟踪剪枝后模型适应过程）和‘如何恢复’（离线蒸馏只覆盖教师前缀，全轨迹在线蒸馏导致学生误差跨轮次累积）。

Method: TAP的输入是长时程智能体交互轨迹（状态/观察、学生生成的推理-动作响应序列）。框架首先对RL训练后的7B密集智能体进行结构化剪枝，初始通道选择基于某种重要性估计（摘要指出一次性估计不足，故TAP改为迭代方式）。核心流程为：
1）从当前学生模型出发，在教师轨迹锚定下执行在线策略恢复：交互环境/前缀由教师轨迹给定，但每一步的响应由学生模型自回归生成；冻结的密集教师对学生的响应前缀进行监督（类似逐前缀蒸馏），从而只纠正学生响应内部分布，不将学生误差传播到后续轮次。
2）恢复目标训练若干步后，在恢复后的学生模型上计算恢复目标的梯度，用梯度对FFN通道重新评分，据此进行下一轮剪枝，迭代地连接通道选择与演化策略。
3）推理时仅部署剪枝后的学生模型，无需教师，直接用于长时程智能体任务。
整体为解决‘剪枝后模型会适应、离线蒸馏覆盖不全、在线全轨迹蒸馏误差累积’三个问题而设计。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "任务成功率保留比例", "value": "99.2% (相比密集7B智能体)", "setting": "ALFWorld，剪除60% FFN通道"}, {"metric": "任务成功率保留比例", "value": "88.0% (相比密集7B智能体)", "setting": "WebShop，剪除60% FFN通道"}], "online_gains": [{"metric": "每个成功任务GPU时间减少", "value": "约22%", "setting": "ALFWorld，剪除60% FFN通道"}, {"metric": "每个成功任务GPU时间减少", "value": "约17%", "setting": "WebShop，剪除60% FFN通道"}]}

Conclusion: 提出首个面向RL训练智能体的结构化剪枝框架TAP，将结构化剪枝与高效在线策略恢复耦合。；轨迹锚定恢复：交互锚定于教师轨迹，但每一步的推理-动作响应由学生生成，冻结的密集教师监督学生的响应前缀，缓解响应内训练-推理不匹配并防止学生诱导偏差跨训练轮次传播。；迭代通道重评分：不采用一次性剪枝，而是在恢复后的学生模型上使用恢复目标的梯度重新评分通道，将迭代通道选择与演化中的策略连接起来。

Abstract: Emerging long-horizon agentic tasks require repeated model calls, worsening the inference cost of already-costly language models. While narrow agentic tasks suggest potential for aggressive model pruning without performance drop, empirical results show existing methods proposed for question answering tasks severely degrade task performance when applied to agentic models. We trace this failure to two decisions: what to prune and how to recover. For pruning, one-shot importance estimates fail to track how the pruned model adapts. For recovery, offline distillation covers only teacher prefixes, while full-trajectory on-policy distillation causes student errors to compound across turns. In this work, we propose Trajectory-Anchored Pruning (TAP), the first structural pruning framework for reinforcement learning (RL)-trained agents. TAP couples structural pruning with efficient on-policy recovery, anchoring interactions to teacher trajectories while allowing the student to generate each reasoning-action response. A frozen dense teacher supervises the student's response prefixes, addressing within-response training-inference mismatch while preventing student-induced deviations from propagating across training turns. Instead of one-shot pruning, TAP re-scores channels using gradients of the recovery objective on the recovered student, connecting iterative channel selection to the evolving policy. With 60% of FFN channels removed, TAP retains 99.2% and 88.0% of the dense 7B agents' task success rates on ALFWorld and WebShop, respectively, while reducing GPU time per successful task by approximately 22% and 17%. These results demonstrate effective structural compression of long-horizon agents under a limited recovery budget.

</details>

Conclusion: 系统比较了生成式（1B/4B/8B因果SLM）与判别式（DeBERTa-V3、ModernBERT）学生模型在多标签主题分配任务上的表现，揭示数据依赖的架构权衡；发现判别式模型在结构化商品评论上更优，而最小1B生成式模型在复杂多轮对话数据上超越判别式基线；验证生成式模型在标签集扩展至112个主题及严重长尾分布下仍保持鲁棒，而判别式基线Macro-F1下降35%；在生产环境中部署了针对商品评论与对话域优化的模型，满足严格延迟要求并带来可量化的业务影响

Abstract: Multi-label topic assignment for user-generated content (UGC) -- including product reviews and buyer-seller conversations -- poses unique scalability challenges in large-scale e-commerce due to informal language, extreme label sparsity, and rapidly evolving taxonomies. While utilizing Large Language Models (LLMs) as labeling oracles to distill ground-truth data has emerged as an industry standard to bypass prohibitive manual annotation costs, determining the optimal, low-latency architecture for the resulting student models remains an open challenge. To address this, we conduct a comprehensive evaluation across Small Language Model (SLM) parameter scales (1B, 4B, and 8B) and architectural paradigms (causal generative versus bidirectional discriminative). Comparing generative text-to-label classifiers against discriminative baselines (DeBERTa-V3 and ModernBERT), our analysis reveals a crucial data-dependent trade-off: while discriminative models outperform ultra-lightweight generative models on structured product reviews, even the smallest 1B generative model surpasses discriminative baselines on complex, multi-turn conversational data. Furthermore, generative models maintain robust performance under massive label-set expansion (up to 112 topics) and severe long-tail distributions, whereas discriminative baselines suffer a 35% drop in Macro-F1 at scale. Finally, we detail the successful production deployment of these optimized models across both product review and conversational domains, demonstrating strict latency compliance and tangible business impact at a global marketplace scale.

</details>

TL;DR: MATCHA 提出联合学习显式的目标×源层映射和层共享特征映射，实现跨模型激活对齐；在 42 对模型上重建更准、检索指标更好，并支持 steering vector 与 probe 的跨模型迁移，揭示了对齐的多对多结构。

Method: MATCHA 学习从源模型逐层激活到目标模型激活的映射。该映射被分解为两个因子：一个层映射，其输出是一个可提取和检查的显式目标×源矩阵；以及一个层共享的特征映射，用于在隐藏空间之间转换特征。与先前工作中预先固定层对应关系（通常按大致相同的相对深度配对层）不同，MATCHA 从提示中联合学习两个因子。在实验中，作者使用了 7 个模型、3 个家族的 42 对模型，并表明 MATCHA 在目标激活重建和检索指标上均优于先前方法。学习到的映射总体上呈深度单调性，但始终是多对多的，即每个目标层从源层的一个带中提取信息。该对齐还支持激活空间干预的迁移，使为一个模型开发的 steering vectors 和 probes 能够迁移到另一个模型。

Conclusion: 将激活对齐分解为显式的目标层到源层矩阵（层映射）和层共享的特征映射，二者联合学习。；从提示中联合学习层映射与特征映射，无需预定义相对深度配对。；发现对齐通常是多对多的：每个目标层依赖源模型中的一组层。；利用学习到的对齐迁移激活空间干预（steering vectors 和 probes）。

Abstract: LLMs are released at a rapid pace, raising a natural question: how do two independently trained models relate, both in which layers correspond and in how features transform between them? We study this by learning an activation alignment, a map from a source model's layerwise activations to a target's. Our method, MATCHA, factors this map into a layer map, whose output is an explicit target-by-source matrix that can be extracted and inspected, and a layer-shared feature map between hidden spaces. Most of prior work fixes the layer correspondence in advance, pairing layers at roughly the same relative depth; in contrast, we learn both factors jointly from prompts. Across 42 pairs of seven models spanning three different families, MATCHA reconstructs the target's activations more faithfully and improves retrieval-based metrics substantially, w.r.t. previous approaches. The recovered maps are broadly monotone in depth but, in contrast with most previous approaches, are consistently many-to-many: each target layer draws on a band of source layers. Our alignments also enable transfer of activation-space interventions, allowing steering vectors and probes developed for one model to transfer to another.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大语言模型的 KV-Cache 过大，阻碍高效部署；现有基于衰减函数的替代注意力机制表达能力有限，且实际退化为类似滑窗的淘汰模式。

Method: Universal Attention 保留标准 RoPE 位置嵌入和 Softmax 注意力，在此基础上引入可端到端训练的复合衰减机制作为注意力得分的调制项。该机制综合多种互补的衰减函数，捕获键的复杂统计与交互，从而自适应地判断哪些 token 对注意力计算的贡献最小。推理时，根据衰减得分淘汰贡献低的 token，实现 KV-Cache 剪枝，且剪枝准则由训练过程自然习得，无需额外启发式规则。架构整体保持与预训练 Transformer 的兼容性，可端到端微调。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "KV-Cache 压缩率", "value": "10×", "setting": "自然语言和合成任务数据"}, {"metric": "KV-Cache 压缩率", "value": "25×", "setting": "长上下文长度 16k"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出一个统一的衰减机制框架，能够捕获复杂的键统计与交互，同时保留富有表达力的 RoPE 位置嵌入和 Softmax 注意力。；设计 Universal Attention，一种高度表达且端到端可训练的架构，其复合衰减机制作为自然自适应剪枝准则。；实现自然语言和合成任务数据上 10× 的 KV-Cache 压缩，并超越最先进基线和未剪枝 oracle 的下游性能；在 16k 长度上达到 25× 压缩。

Abstract: The large KV-cache size of modern LLMs creates a barrier to efficient deployment. Recent work has explored replacing attention layers' RoPE positional embeddings with alternative decay-based mechanisms, which can then be used to prune KV-cache during inference. However, these decay functions have limited expressivity, and in practice devolve into sliding-window-like eviction patterns. In this work, we propose a unifying framework for complementary and novel decay mechanisms, capturing complex key statistics and interactions while preserving expressive RoPE embeddings and Softmax attention. The resulting Universal Attention is a highly expressive and end-to-end trainable architecture, whose composite decay mechanism acts as a natural, $\textit{adaptive}$ pruning criterion, removing tokens that contribute least to attention computation. Experimentally, Universal Attention achieves state-of-the-art $10\times$ compression on natural language and synthetic task data, while $\textit{improving}$ downstream performance compared to both state-of-the-art baselines and unpruned oracles. It further demonstrates superior long-context generalization with unprecedented $25\times$ compression at length 16k.

</details>


### [55] [Task-Oriented Key-Layer KV Communication for Efficient Latent Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08820)
*Dongsen Zhang,Peipei Li,Zekun Li,Wenjun Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KITE 提出免训练的任务导向关键层 KV 通信框架，将多智能体潜在通信目标从发送端状态保真转为接收端任务充分性，仅传输关键层潜在工作记忆并以该层作为潜在推理入口，在七个基准上通信量减少 28-36 倍、最高 3 倍推理加速、准确率最高提升 23.3 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 KV 的潜在通信以发送端状态保真为目标，导致通信与计算开销大并引入冗余信息，如何在接收端任务充分性目标下高效传输潜在工作记忆。

Method: KITE 是一个免训练框架，用于任务导向的关键层 KV 通信。其输入是多智能体协作过程中发送方 LLM 的内部 KV 表示。框架首先通过接收端轨迹失真准则识别出一个任务有效的关键层：以接收方在任务上的轨迹失真程度作为选择依据，找到对接收端任务充分性贡献最大的层。然后只传输与该关键层关联的潜在工作记忆（latent working memory），而非全层 KV，从而大幅降低通信量。接收方以同一关键层作为自回归潜在推理的入口，直接在该层的潜在空间中进行后续推理，避免自然语言通信的高推理成本。整个流程无需额外训练，推理时按关键层进行 KV 传输与潜在推理。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "通信量", "value": "减少 28-36 倍", "setting": "与全层 KV 通信相比，七个基准、两个模型家族、三种模型规模"}, {"metric": "端到端推理加速", "value": "最高 3 倍", "setting": "与全层 KV 通信相比，七个基准、两个模型家族、三种模型规模"}, {"metric": "准确率", "value": "最高提升 23.3 个百分点", "setting": "与全层 KV 通信相比，七个基准、两个模型家族、三种模型规模"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将潜在通信目标从发送端状态保真重新定义为接收端任务充分性；提出免训练的 KITE 框架，通过接收端轨迹失真准则识别任务有效的关键层；仅传输关键层对应的潜在工作记忆，并以该层作为自回归潜在推理的入口

Abstract: Large language model-based multi-agent systems improve complex problem solving through collaboration, while latent communication directly transmits model internal states to avoid the high inference costs of natural language. However, existing KV-based latent communication methods prioritize sender-side state fidelity, leading to substantial communication and computation overhead and potentially introducing redundant information. To address these limitations, we revisit latent communication from a task-oriented perspective, shifting its objective from sender-side state fidelity to receiver-side task sufficiency. Under this formulation, we propose KITE, a training-free framework for task-oriented key-layer KV communication. KITE identifies a task-effective key layer using a receiver trajectory distortion criterion, transmits only the latent working memory associated with the key layer, and further uses the same layer as the entry point for autoregressive latent reasoning. Experiments on seven benchmarks across two model families and three model scales show that, compared with full-layer KV communication, KITE reduces communication volume by 28-36$\times$, achieves up to 3$\times$ end-to-end inference speedup, and improves accuracy by up to 23.3 percentage points.

</details>


### [56] [Just for FUNS: LLM-Guided Spatio-Temporal Graph Node Generation for Forecasting Unobserved Node States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08818)
*Shuhao Li,Weidong Yang,Changan Liu,Wei Zhuo,Yingbo Zhou,Fan Zhang,Siqiang Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GenST，利用LLM提取节点语义特征，结合ST-VAE与生成Transformer，实现未观测节点状态的条件生成，在零样本预测任务中显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器网络空间覆盖不全时，如何对无历史观测节点进行未来状态预测（FUNS）。

Method: GenST框架将FUNS建模为时空图上的条件生成任务。输入包括节点描述文本（功能区、路网结构）、地理坐标、邻域上下文以及时空观测数据。首先使用预训练LLM（微调后）从节点描述中提取语义特征；同时，时空VAE将时空动态压缩至潜在空间。然后，生成式Transformer（GenT）以噪声为起点，在多模态条件（语义、坐标、邻域）引导下，迭代重建未观测节点的未来状态。训练目标为条件生成损失（如变分下界与重建损失的组合），推理时直接采样生成未来状态。该架构通过LLM语义特征补偿缺失的时空信号，解决无历史节点的预测问题。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "预测性能", "value": "显著优于现有基线", "setting": "六个交通和两个非交通数据集的零样本预测任务"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将FUNS问题重新定义为时空图上的条件生成任务；利用预训练LLM提取节点语义特征（功能区、路网结构）作为语义桥梁；两阶段生成架构：ST-VAE压缩时空动态 + GenT从噪声生成未来状态，融合语义、坐标和邻域上下文

Abstract: Spatio-temporal forecasting is a cornerstone of logistics, urban planning, and intelligent transportation systems. However, constrained by deployment costs and maintenance resources, sensor networks often lack comprehensive spatial coverage, rendering Forecast Unobserved Node States (FUNS) a critical yet formidable challenge. Conventional models rely on historical observations and typically falter when encountering nodes without prior records. To address this, we redefine the problem as a conditional generation task on spatio-temporal graphs and propose GenST, a framework that introduces Large Language Models (LLMs) as a semantic bridge, leveraging a pre-trained LLM fine-tuned to extract rich semantic features from node descriptions, such as functional zones and road network structures, to compensate for missing spatio-temporal signals. Specifically, we design a two-stage generative architecture: a Spatio-Temporal VAE first compresses spatio-temporal dynamics into a latent space, followed by a Generative Transformer (GenT) that reconstructs the future states of unobserved nodes from noise, guided by multi-modal conditions including semantics, geographic coordinates, and neighborhood contexts. Experiments on six traffic and two non-traffic datasets show GenST significantly outperforms existing baselines in zero-shot prediction tasks, demonstrating the practical potential of semantic-guided generation for mitigating spatio-temporal data sparsity.

</details>


### [57] [KVFetch: Temporal Prefetching for the Missing Half of KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08811)
*Linfeng Dong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KVFetch 指出现有 KV 缓存压缩只实现按内容关联查找、忽略按位置顺序遍历，导致需要逐字复制时出现顺序遗忘。它提出免训练、可插拔的时序预取框架：将被逐出候选降级到量化冷层，用单调读指针检测复制行为，并按位置预取后继到固定热层槽位而不增加注意力成本。RULER-16K 等预算控制下逐字复制从 0.8 恢复至 78.4，13 任务平均提升 +8.4；LongBench 上通道休眠且无额外开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 KV 缓存压缩方法均按内容相关性决定保留或召回，忽略了缓存同样需要按位置进行顺序遍历这一访问模式，导致在需要逐字复制的任务中发生顺序遗忘，压缩后模型无法从中途续写序列。

Method: KVFetch 是一个免训练、可直接替换的框架，作用于任意基于评分的 KV 缓存压缩器。输入为长上下文推理时生成的 KV 缓存与当前查询。现有压缩器在内容相关性评分后逐出低分候选；KVFetch 不改变评分本身，而是为被逐出的候选增加一条时序召回通道。具体地：1）将被逐出的候选 KV 对降级到量化冷层（cold tier），以低精度存储，避免完全丢弃；2）在解码过程中维护一个单调读指针，利用注意力读出模式检测模型是否正在进行连续复制（即按位置逐 token 复现上下文序列）；3）一旦检测到活跃复制，就按位置后继从冷层预取（prefetch）到固定大小的热层（hot tier）槽位，使后继 token 的 KV 在需要时已位于高精度热层；4）热层槽位数量固定，因此预取不会增加注意力计算量，仅复用已有槽位。训练目标：无（training-free），不引入额外参数或微调。推理流程：标准自回归解码，在每步注意力的同时并行执行读指针更新、活跃复制检测与后继预取；当任务不需要顺序访问时，检测器不触发，冷层预取通道保持休眠，因此不带来质量或成本影响。该设计直接解决顺序遗忘：内容相关性压缩会保留序列头部而丢弃延续部分导致逐字复制断裂，时序通道通过按位置预取延续部分来恢复顺序遍历能力。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "逐字复制得分", "value": "0.8 -> 78.4", "setting": "RULER-16K，等预算控制（iso-budget control）"}, {"metric": "13 任务平均分", "value": "+8.4", "setting": "RULER-16K，等预算控制"}, {"metric": "顺序访问任务上的增益", "value": "增益集中在需要顺序访问的任务上；LongBench 上无顺序访问任务时通道休眠、无成本", "setting": "RULER-16K 与 LongBench 基准评测"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 指出只实现内容关联查找的压缩器在结构上不完整，缓存还需支持按位置的顺序遍历，并定义顺序遗忘这一失败模式。；提出 KVFetch：免训练、可插拔框架，为任意基于评分的压缩器开辟时序召回通道。；设计冷热分层机制：将被逐出的候选降级到量化冷层，通过单调读指针检测正在进行的复制行为，并将位置后继预取到固定大小的热层槽位。；在不增加注意力计算成本的前提下实现预取，且在无需顺序访问的任务上通道保持休眠、不产生额外开销。

Abstract: As context windows scale to tens or hundreds of thousands of tokens, KV cache compression has become essential for efficient LLM inference. Existing methods fall into three families: score-based eviction, summary compensation, and offload-and-recall. Yet all three decide what to keep or recall by content relevance to the current query. We show this shared design is structurally incomplete. A cache supports two access modes: associative lookup by content and sequential traversal by position; current compressors implement only the first. The gap matters in practice: retrieval-augmented generation, code completion, and structured-data extraction all require the model to reproduce identifiers, field values, or code tokens verbatim from the context. Under compression, content-based eviction retains the head of such a sequence but discards its continuation, causing verbatim copying to break irreversibly midway, a failure we call sequential forgetting. This failure resists better scoring, larger budgets, summary compensation, and dynamic re-scoring; it is the dominant source of remaining quality loss under compression. We propose KVFetch, a training-free, drop-in framework that opens a temporal recall channel for any score-based compressor. It demotes evicted candidates to a quantized cold tier, detects active copying through a monotone read pointer, and prefetches positional successors into fixed-size hot-tier slots without increasing attention cost. On RULER-16K under an iso-budget control, KVFetch recovers verbatim copying from 0.8 to 78.4 and raises the 13-task average by +8.4, with gains concentrating on tasks that require sequential access. On LongBench, where no task requires sequential access, the channel remains dormant and imposes no cost.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>
