### [58] [Does Document Structure Help Dense Retrieval? A Placebo-Controlled Ablation of Four Mechanisms Across Two Corpora](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10170)
*Andrey Kuehlkamp,Priscila Correa Saboia Moreira,Samuel Rund*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究以安慰剂对照消融统一评估四种文档结构处理对稠密检索的影响，发现结构对齐切分加真实标题路径有效且源于内容而非 token 扰动，而朴素两段层次检索反而损害检索；为 RAG 检索管线设计提供了控制混淆的实证依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档结构处理（结构对齐切分、LLM 生成上下文、标题路径元数据、层次化两段检索）在稠密检索中的真实贡献不明，已有研究未控制“任何前置文本都会扰动 chunk 嵌入”这一共同混淆因素。

Method: 论文评估的是 RAG 稠密检索前端处理流程：输入文档首先被切分为 chunk，四种结构处理机制分别作用于切分与嵌入阶段——(1) 结构对齐切分，按文档标题层级对齐到语义边界；(2) LLM 生成 chunk 上下文，在 chunk 前拼接 LLM 生成的上下文；(3) 标题路径元数据，在 chunk 前拼接真实标题路径；(4) 层次化两段检索，先召回 section 再召回 chunk。所有条件匹配 chunk 大小以避免长度混淆，并加入语义中性安慰剂：结构合法但跨文档打乱的标题路径，用于控制任何前置文本对嵌入的扰动。检索评估采用覆盖感知 nDCG，四个预注册对比通过文档聚类 bootstrap 与 Holm 校正检验显著性。实验在两个语料上进行：200 篇 Wikipedia Featured Articles（951 条查询）和 1,585 篇 QASPER 论文（4,303 个问题）。论文未披露嵌入模型架构、训练目标或推理服务细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "覆盖感知 nDCG@10 提升", "value": "+0.022", "setting": "Wikipedia Featured Articles，结构对齐 chunk 加真实标题路径对比上下文化固定窗口，951 条查询"}, {"metric": "覆盖感知 nDCG@10 提升", "value": "+0.012", "setting": "QASPER，结构对齐 chunk 加真实标题路径对比上下文化固定窗口，4303 个问题"}, {"metric": "覆盖感知 nDCG@10 提升", "value": "+0.010", "setting": "Wikipedia Featured Articles，对比安慰剂（打乱的标题路径）"}, {"metric": "覆盖感知 nDCG@10 提升", "value": "+0.016", "setting": "QASPER，对比安慰剂（打乱的标题路径）"}, {"metric": "覆盖感知 nDCG@10 下降", "value": "-0.033", "setting": "Wikipedia Featured Articles，朴素两段层次检索对比基线，归因于第一阶段 section 召回"}, {"metric": "覆盖感知 nDCG@10 下降", "value": "-0.015", "setting": "QASPER，朴素两段层次检索对比基线，归因于第一阶段 section 召回"}, {"metric": "效应量 dz", "value": "0.06-0.11", "setting": "两个语料上 Holm 显著但效应较小"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 在统一协议下隔离四种文档结构处理机制进行消融，并匹配各条件下的 chunk 大小；引入语义中性安慰剂（结构合法但跨文档打乱的标题路径）以控制嵌入扰动混淆；使用覆盖感知 nDCG 评估，并通过文档聚类 bootstrap 与 Holm 校正检验四个预注册对比；在两个差异较大的语料（Wikipedia Featured Articles 与 QASPER）上验证结论一致性

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation systems increasingly rely on document-structure treatments: structure-aligned chunking, LLM-generated chunk contexts, heading-path metadata, and hierarchical two-stage retrieval. Separate studies support each on different corpora, embedders, and metrics, and none control for a shared confound: any text prepended to a chunk perturbs its embedding. We present a mechanism-isolating ablation testing all four treatments under one protocol, matching chunk sizes across conditions and adding a semantically null placebo---heading paths that are structurally valid but shuffled across documents. We score retrieval with a coverage-aware nDCG and test four pre-registered contrasts via document-clustered bootstrap with Holm correction, on two distant corpora: 200 Wikipedia Featured Articles (951 queries) and 1,585 QASPER papers (4,303 questions). Organization helps, and the cause is content, not tokens: structure-aligned chunks with real heading paths beat contextualized fixed windows (+0.022 / +0.012 cov-nDCG@10) and the placebo (+0.010 / +0.016). Naive two-stage hierarchical retrieval hurts (-0.033 / -0.015), traceable to first-stage section recall. Gold structure beats LLM-induced structure on Wikipedia but not on QASPER. Effects are small ($dz$ 0.06-0.11) but Holm-significant and consistent across corpora.

</details>


### [59] [Training with Missed Targets in Generative Recommendation: Separating Supervision from Probability Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10124)
*Xuesi Wang,Yangbin Shi,Xiaolin Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究生成式推荐中训练时追加遗漏目标对重排排序的影响，通过三种匹配损失分离监督与概率竞争，实验表明竞争会损害排序，建议按生成器评估是否采用追加目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将生成推荐器在检索阶段遗漏的目标样本追加到重排训练列表中，会同时改变已检索目标的权重、引入追加目标的监督信号，并使两组目标在概率空间中竞争，导致返回物品排序性能下降。

Method: 论文提出的框架以生成式推荐器为基础，检索阶段返回有限候选集，重排阶段对候选进行排序。训练时，将检索遗漏的观测目标追加到重排训练列表中。为解耦追加操作的影响，构建三种匹配损失：第一种保持已检索目标权重固定，仅引入追加目标监督；第二种在两组内训练但分别归一化，防止训练专用目标与推理候选竞争；第三种为完整损失，包含追加目标监督和组间竞争。实验使用OneRec模型和本地训练的Amazon生成器，比较不同损失下返回物品排序性能。推理时仅对原始候选排序，追加目标不参与。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "FT-NDCG", "value": "7.8--22.2%", "setting": "Amazon Video Games数据集上四组预设比较，移除追加目标竞争后相对于基线的提升。"}, {"metric": "FT-NDCG损失", "value": "1.7%", "setting": "开发集规则在保留类别上拒绝追加目标训练，避免的性能损失。"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 构建三种匹配损失函数，分别固定已检索目标权重、独立引入追加目标监督和组间竞争。；提出中间损失函数，对已检索组和追加组分别归一化，防止仅用于训练的目标与推理候选竞争。；通过OneRec和本地训练的Amazon生成器进行实验，验证竞争对返回物品排序的伤害。；提出开发集规则，根据生成器决定是否采用追加目标训练。

Abstract: Generative recommenders return a limited candidate set and may omit observed targets before reranking. A training strategy appends these missed targets to reranker training lists, although inference still ranks only original candidates. This operation simultaneously changes retrieved-target weight, adds supervision over appended targets, and makes the two groups compete for probability. An append/no-append comparison therefore cannot explain changes in returned-item rankings. We construct three matched losses that hold retrieved-target weight fixed while introducing appended-target supervision and group competition separately. The intermediate loss trains within both groups but normalizes them separately, preventing training-only targets from competing with inference candidates. Experiments with a released OneRec model and locally trained Amazon generators show that this competition can harm returned-item ranking. In four prespecified Amazon Video Games comparisons, removing it improved full-target normalized discounted cumulative gain (FT-NDCG) by 7.8--22.2\%; 95\% intervals over users and three of four intervals over training runs excluded zero. A conservative development-set rule selected appended-target training for two of three generators in one held-out category and rejected it for all three in another, avoiding a 1.7\% loss. Candidate completion should therefore be evaluated for each generator rather than applied automatically.

</details>


### [60] [The Impact of Backbone Evolution on LLM-Based Relevance Assessments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09820)
*Chuting Yu,Guido Zuccon,Teerapong Leelanupab*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文挑战了LLM版本越新、相关性判断越好的假设。通过固定提示词，在Gemini、GPT、Qwen、Llama的连续版本上评估UMBRELA和EXAM两种相关性判断范式，发现新版本并不总是带来更好的与人类判断的一致性，且聚合性能提升不保证判断稳定性。研究警示：为某一版本设计和验证的提示词在模型更新后可能不再适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在LLM-based relevance judging中，固定提示词时，骨干模型版本更新是否总能带来与人类判断更高的一致性，以及这种一致性是否意味着判断的稳定性。

Method: 论文研究LLM作为相关性判断器的评估框架。输入为查询-文档对，使用固定提示词：UMBRELA（单提示词直接输出相关性分数）和EXAM（基于评分细则的结构化提示）。对每个查询-文档对，通过多个连续版本的商业模型（Gemini、GPT）和开源模型（Qwen、Llama）进行推理，得到相关性判断。然后计算与人类判断的一致性（如Cohen's kappa或准确率），并对比不同版本之间的判断结果差异，分析聚合性能变化与判断稳定性（相同对上的判断翻转）。无涉及模型训练或系统部署的架构描述。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 系统性地跨商业模型（Gemini、GPT）和开源模型（Qwen、Llama）的多个连续版本，在固定提示词下评估LLM相关性判断器的表现，覆盖单提示词（UMBRELA）和基于rubric的提示词（EXAM）两种范式。；区分了聚合性能（如与人类判断的一致率）与判断稳定性，发现聚合性能相似或提升并不保证早期版本的正确判断被后续版本保留。；初步探究了导致性能回退的潜在驱动因素（如模型家族内部更新时的行为变化）。

Abstract: LLMs are evolving rapidly, with newer models offering stronger capabilities. This suggests that in LLM-based relevance judging, more capable models will achieve higher agreement with human judgements under the same prompt. We challenge this understanding by investigating the behavior of LLM-based relevance judges under backbone evolution. Keeping the prompts fixed, we evaluate a representative single-prompt (UMBRELA) and a rubric-based prompt (EXAM) across sequential model versions of commercial (Gemini, GPT) and open-weight (Qwen, Llama) models. Overall, we find no consistent evidence that newer versions lead to better relevance judges. Crucially, similar or improved aggregate performance does not imply judgment stability: correct judgements made by an earlier version of an LLM backbone are not necessarily preserved by later versions. We investigate the potential drivers of these regressions. Our findings caution against the assumption that judging prompts designed and validated for one backbone version will perform equivalently or better when the model is updated, even within the same family.

</details>


### [61] [Finding the Right Balance: Relevance and Diversity in LLM Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09412)
*Guillaume Brouillette,Faustin Kagabo,Usef Faghihi,Nadia Ghazzali*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究RAG检索多样化何时有益：发现效果取决于候选池冗余度与查询所需证据数量，提出由已有嵌入计算的查询自适应多样化规则和带最近邻回退的RNG-Score几何重排器，在干净池上避免损害、在多证据冗余池上获得增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索多样化在RAG中何时有益、何时有害尚不明确；需要根据候选池冗余度与查询所需证据数量自适应决定是否多样化

Method: 论文面向RAG检索阶段。输入为查询与其候选文档池，使用现有嵌入模型得到查询和文档的向量表示；进行最近邻top-k候选选择。核心判断模块从最近邻top-k中计算有效不同文档数量(基于已有嵌入)，与查询的证据需求比较；若有效不同文档数低于证据需求，则触发多样化重排，否则退化为普通最近邻检索。提出RNG-Score几何重排器，以精确最近邻作为回退，其margin反映重复结构，用于辅助检测候选池冗余。训练/优化方面未披露具体的模型训练流程，规则与重排器均基于现有嵌入和几何关系计算。推理路径为：嵌入→最近邻top-k→有效不同文档估计→条件多样化/回退。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "多样化对相关性、证据覆盖与答案质量的影响", "value": "在干净候选池上多样化会损害相关性、证据覆盖和答案质量；在多证据任务且冗余导致最近邻选择重复段落时变得有益", "setting": "受控近重复注入与生产风格重叠分块实验；跨数据集与编码器迁移"}, {"metric": "查询自适应规则捕获的增益", "value": "捕获大部分可达到增益", "setting": "基于已有嵌入计算；自动在单证据查询上退化为最近邻检索"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发现多样化效果主要取决于候选池冗余度，并与查询所需不同证据片段数量一致；提出查询自适应规则：仅当最近邻top-k中有效不同文档数低于查询证据需求时才进行多样化；该规则由已有嵌入计算，不需要额外模型，能捕获大部分可达到增益；提出RNG-Score几何重排器，带精确最近邻回退，其margin可指示重复结构

Abstract: Retrieval diversification is widely available in retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) frameworks, yet prior studies disagree on whether it improves retrieval and answer quality. We show that its effectiveness varies primarily with candidate-pool redundancy, in a pattern consistent with the number of distinct evidence pieces a query requires. Using controlled near-duplicate injection and production-style overlapping chunking, we find that diversification harms relevance, evidence coverage and answer quality on clean pools, but becomes beneficial on multi-evidence tasks when redundancy causes nearest-neighbor retrieval to select repeated passages. We therefore introduce a query-adaptive rule that diversifies only when the effective number of distinct documents in the nearest-neighbor top-$k$ selection falls below the query's evidence requirement. Computed from existing embeddings, the rule captures most of the achievable gain, transfers across datasets and encoders and automatically reduces to nearest-neighbor retrieval for single-evidence queries. We also introduce RNG-Score, a geometric reranker with an exact nearest-neighbor fallback whose margin indicates duplicate structure. Overall, we conclude that diversification should be used selectively, based on observable redundancy and evidence requirements.

</details>


### [62] [Quantize by Drift: Label-Free Mixed-Precision Post-Training Quantization for Text Embedders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09227)
*Hyojung Han,Jongmin Kim,Seung-Hun Jeon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出无标签的量化敏感度信号——输出表示漂移，用于文本嵌入模型的混合精度训练后量化。在五个开发模型上配置级漂移与留出检索质量的宏 Spearman 达 0.911，跨语料与检索域可迁移；三个密封模型上预注册方向性假设 3/3 成立，但在主预算下数值上低于同预算均匀量化（-0.99、-0.85、-1.01 点）。作者结论为输出漂移是稳健的粗粒度敏感度信号而非普遍最优的分配目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合精度训练后量化需要逐模块的敏感度信号，而文本嵌入模型中最直观的敏感度（模块量化造成的检索质量损失）依赖部署中通常缺失的相关性标签，因此需要一种无标签的敏感度替代信号。

Method: 方法流程为：给定一个全精度文本嵌入模型与一份无标签校准语料，先对语料做全精度编码得到参考输出嵌入（部署中检索器实际用于排序的表示）。随后逐一选择单个模块将其量化（保持其他模块为全精度），用该混合配置重新编码同一语料，计算新输出嵌入相对参考嵌入的偏移量，作为该模块的量化敏感度——即量化引起的表示漂移。该观测指标直接作用于部署输出表示，因此与检索质量损失同处一个可观测空间。得到各模块漂移后，方法在硬性打包字节预算约束下执行一次加性分配：按漂移敏感度为各模块分配量化精度，不做标签监督、不做搜索式优化。为验证该信号的泛化性，论文检验了敏感度跨校准语料与跨检索域的迁移性、模块漂移在排序意义上可组合但数值上不可加，以及引入相关性标签导出的敏感度是否能带来一致增益。最终在三个在方法、基线与假设冻结并密封前未触碰的嵌入模型上做预注册方向性假设检验，并与 LieQ 准则及同预算均匀精度比较。结论是输出漂移是稳健的粗粒度敏感度信号，而非普遍最优的分配目标：它避免了迁移的符号几何自适应带来的灾难性失败，并在压力预算下均匀量化崩塌时仍可用，但围绕强均匀工作点的细粒度再分配仍未解决。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "宏平均 Spearman 相关系数（配置级漂移 vs 留出检索质量排序）", "value": "0.911", "setting": "五个开发用文本嵌入模型上的配置级混合精度方案排序"}, {"metric": "检索质量（相对同预算均匀量化的差值）", "value": "-0.99 / -0.85 / -1.01 点", "setting": "主预算下，三个密封嵌入模型，漂移分配 vs 同预算均匀精度"}, {"metric": "预注册方向性假设（对 LieQ 准则）成立数", "value": "3/3", "setting": "主预算；另在 2/3 个模型上优于两侧 LieQ steelman"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出以量化引起的输出表示漂移（representation drift）作为无标签敏感度信号：量化单个模块后重新编码语料，测量输出嵌入相对全精度位置的偏移；将敏感度观测点定位于稠密检索器实际排序所用的部署输出表示，而非内部激活或权重；在硬性打包字节预算下用漂移做一次加性分配（additive allocation），不依赖标签与搜索；系统性验证敏感度对校准语料与检索域的迁移性、模块漂移的排序一致性组合性，以及相关性导出的敏感度是否带来额外价值；预注册并密封式对比实验：以 LieQ 准则为基线假设，在三个未曾触碰的嵌入模型上验证方向性假设

Abstract: Mixed-precision post-training quantization needs a per-module sensitivity signal; for a text embedder the obvious one -- the retrieval quality a module costs when quantized -- needs relevance labels that deployments rarely have. We measure a label-free substitute: quantization-induced representation drift, obtained by quantizing one module, re-encoding the corpus, and recording how far the output embeddings moved from their full-precision positions. What is specific is the observable: the deployed output representation a dense retriever ranks with. Across five development embedders, configuration-level drift orders sampled mixed-precision plans against held-out retrieval quality at a macro Spearman of 0.911, the sensitivity transports across calibration corpora and retrieval domains in the usable regime, module drifts compose rank-consistently but not numerically, and relevance-derived sensitivity adds no consistent value. The method is one additive allocation under a hard packed-byte budget, with no labels and no search. On three embedders held untouched until method, baselines and hypotheses were frozen and sealed, the pre-registered directional hypothesis against the prior LieQ criterion holds (3/3 at the main budget, no collapse) and drift scores above a two-sided LieQ steelman in 2/3; but at the main budget drift is numerically lower than same-budget uniform precision on all three (-0.99, -0.85, -1.01 points), having reduced module and whole-model drift as designed. Output drift is thus a robust coarse sensitivity signal, not a universally optimal allocation objective: it avoids the catastrophic failures of the transferred signed-geometry adaptation and can remain usable at stressed budgets where uniform collapses, but fine-grained redistribution around a strong uniform operating point remains unresolved.

</details>


### [63] [What Transfers from a VLM Teacher? Comparing Supervision Signals for Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09177)
*Saba Sturua,Han Xiao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文比较了视觉文档检索中VLM教师监督信号的不同使用方式，发现将教师用于评判困难负样本和分数蒸馏能显著提升nDCG@5（55.2→62.6/63.0），优于丰富正样本的方法，并揭示标签不完整是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在视觉文档检索的对比学习中，正样本标签过于粗略且负样本可能实际相关，如何有效利用VLM教师信号提升检索器训练。

Method: 论文研究视觉文档检索的对比学习框架。输入为查询和文档页面图像，检索器（学生模型）通过对比损失训练：每个查询匹配一个标注为正的页面，并与挖掘的负样本页面推远。近期方法通过视觉语言模型（VLM）教师丰富正样本（如传递注意力或描述）。本文提出将教师用于评判学生挖掘的候选负样本：教师对候选页面进行相关性评分（例如使用0-100评分提示），这些评分被蒸馏到学生模型中（分数蒸馏），或教师直接标记困难负样本用于训练。此外，还探索了描述对齐（教师生成页面描述用于对齐）和注意力接地（但效果不显著）。训练目标结合对比损失和蒸馏损失（教师评分或描述）。推理时，学生检索器独立使用，无需教师。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "ViDoRe v2 nDCG@5", "value": "55.2 -> 62.6", "setting": "教师判定困难负样本"}, {"metric": "ViDoRe v2 nDCG@5", "value": "55.2 -> 63.0", "setting": "分数蒸馏"}, {"metric": "ViDoRe v2 nDCG@5", "value": "提升2.6点", "setting": "描述对齐（description alignment）"}, {"metric": "ViDoRe v2 nDCG@5", "value": "教师判断相比最佳无教师规则增加4.1点", "setting": "相同训练计算下，从同一挖掘池选择四个候选"}, {"metric": "ViDoRe v3 nDCG@5", "value": "教师判断相比最佳无教师规则增加1.7点", "setting": "相同训练计算下"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 提出将VLM教师用于评判学生挖掘的候选负样本（教师判定的困难负样本）而非仅丰富正样本；引入分数蒸馏（score distillation）利用教师对候选的评分进行蒸馏；通过人类标注审计揭示挖掘负样本中大量相关但未被标注，解释了标签不完整性问题；对比教师监督与无教师规则（如同等计算下的阈值筛选）的增益，量化教师判断的附加价值

Abstract: Visual document retrievers are trained contrastively: each query is matched to one page labelled relevant - the positive - and pushed away from negatives, pages presumed irrelevant. Recent methods distil a vision-language model (VLM) teacher into the retriever by enriching that positive, transferring the teacher's attention over it or a description of it. We ask whether the teacher is better spent on the other side, judging the candidates the retriever mines as negatives, which the label says nothing about. With student, data, optimizer and evaluation fixed, teacher-judged hard negatives and score distillation raise ViDoRe v2 nDCG@5 from 55.2 to 62.6 and 63.0; description alignment, as adapted here, gains 2.6 points and attention grounding nothing measurable. Against teacher-free rules that select four candidates from the same mined pool at identical training compute, the best of which is the positive-aware threshold current systems use, the teacher's judgement adds 4.1 points on v2 and 1.7 on v3. This is consistent with how incomplete the labels are. Annotators judge about two of a query's four top-ranked mined candidates relevant, none of them labelled, so training pushes the retriever away from relevant pages treated as negatives. What reaches the student is coarse: under a greedily decoded 0-100 rating prompt, 82% of the teacher's ratings come back at one end of the scale or the other, and a relevant/irrelevant partition keeps most of the distillation gain. A ten-annotator audit places the teacher within the range of variation among human annotators, and finds it reliable where a query has a single determinate answer. We release the code, the teacher's 3.3M judgements and page descriptions, the mined pools, the human audit and the trained adapters at this https URL .

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何将非结构化、领域特定的文本转化为结构化知识，并支持可信的检索与推理，同时提升检索精度、减少幻觉并实现可解释的领域AI系统。

Method: 该论文提出一个端到端的可部署模块化架构，用于领域特定结构化知识检索与推理。输入为领域非结构化文本。首先通过半自动语料策展和语义结构化处理，使用Binary Bleed方法加速NMF低秩搜索，并采用HNMFk自动选择潜在特征数量，生成层次化主题分类体系，该体系由领域专家指导以保证可解释性。这些表示填充到一个类型化知识图谱和一个语义对齐的向量存储中，两者通过事件驱动基底同步。检索阶段，T-SRAG动态路由查询到不同的检索路径（可能是知识图谱、向量存储或混合路径），同时对比对齐模块将文档和查询嵌入映射到层次化主题结构，提高语义保真度并减少幻觉。推理阶段，基于张量的链接预测在知识图谱中识别和补全缺失链接，支持基于引用结构的推理。整个架构强调可解释性和模块化，适合领域特定部署。但摘要未提供具体模块的数学模型、训练目标、损失函数或推理服务流程的细节。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: Binary Bleed：一种适配的二分搜索方法，降低非负矩阵分解（NMF）中低秩搜索的复杂度。；HNMFk：层次化非负矩阵分解，自动选择潜在特征数量，由领域专家指导生成可解释的分类体系。；事件驱动架构：同步类型化知识图谱与包含潜在特征的语义对齐向量存储。；T-SRAG：张量结构化检索增强生成，能够动态路由查询到不同检索路径。；对比对齐：将文档和查询嵌入映射到层次化主题结构，提高语义保真度并减少幻觉。；基于张量的链接预测：补全知识图谱中的缺失链接，支持基于引用结构的推理。

Abstract: This dissertation presents a scalable architecture for transforming unstructured, domain-specific text into structured knowledge for retrieval and reasoning. It integrates semi-automatic corpus curation, semantic structuring, retrieval, and inference into an interpretable pipeline. The research introduces Binary Bleed, an adapted binary search method that reduces low-rank search complexity for Non-negative Matrix Factorization (NMF), and Hierarchical NMF with automatic latent feature selection (HNMFk), a depth-adaptive topic modeling method that produces interpretable taxonomies guided by subject matter experts. These representations populate a typed Knowledge Graph and a semantically aligned Vector Store containing extracted latent features, synchronized through an event-driven substrate. Tensor-Structured Retrieval-Augmented Generation (T-SRAG) dynamically routes queries across retrieval paths. Contrastive alignment maps document and query embeddings to hierarchical topic structures to improve semantic fidelity and reduce hallucinations. Beyond retrieval, tensor-based link prediction identifies and completes missing links in the Knowledge Graph, supporting inference grounded in citation structure. Applications across cybersecurity, law, materials science, and healthcare demonstrate improvements in retrieval precision, early trend detection, hypothesis generation, and hallucination mitigation. The dissertation provides a deployable, modular foundation for trustworthy, domain-specific AI systems that retrieve and reason over structured knowledge.

</details>

### [65] [Few Bits, One Law: Toward W2A4KV2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09202)
*Kai Yi,Tarek Elgamal,Sruthikesh Surineni,Vignesh Vivekraja,Soumyadeep Ghosh,Steven Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CanonQ提出统一量化感知训练框架，通过将张量规范化到共享规范坐标并复用冻结高斯码本，实现权重、激活和KV缓存的联合极端低位宽压缩（W2A4KV2），在LLaMA3等模型上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在权重、激活和KV缓存联合量化时，不同源的张量分布差异和量化误差在网络中相互耦合，导致极端低位宽下精度严重下降。

Method: CanonQ是一个统一的量化感知训练框架。输入为预训练LLM的权重、激活和KV缓存张量。首先，通过固定旋转和能量归一化将异构张量源映射到规范坐标，使不同层和模型的张量分布对齐；然后，使用冻结的高斯参考码本在所有层和模型间共享量化码本。联合训练阶段，网络在统一的标量/向量接口下适应权重、激活和缓存量化的耦合误差，训练目标包含量化感知损失。理论上给出了冻结码本迁移误差与局部任务损失的界限，并推导了归一化感知的直通雅可比以关联量化失真与梯度偏差。推理时，模型使用量化后的权重、激活和KV缓存进行前向计算，实现极端位宽（如W2A4KV2）的高效推理。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "WikiText-2 perplexity", "value": "最高降低14.28倍", "setting": "LLaMA3-1B/3B/8B, W2A4KV2"}, {"metric": "零样本平均准确率", "value": "最高提升57.9%", "setting": "LLaMA3-1B/3B/8B, W2A4KV2"}, {"metric": "HumanEval pass@1相对提升", "value": "41.7%", "setting": "MobileLLM-Pro-1B指令微调, W2A16KV16"}, {"metric": "GSM8K exact match相对提升", "value": "39.1%", "setting": "MobileLLM-Pro-1B指令微调, W2A16KV16"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 分离源规范化与任务感知适应的CanonQ框架，使用固定旋转和能量归一化将异构张量映射到规范坐标，实现冻结高斯参考码本跨层跨模型复用。；统一的标量/向量接口联合训练，适应权重、激活和缓存量化耦合误差。；冻结码本迁移误差与局部任务损失的界限，以及归一化感知直通雅可比，将量化失真与梯度偏差关联。

Abstract: Extreme low-bit LLM compression is most challenging when weights, activations, and KV caches are quantized together: their distributions differ, and quantization errors interact throughout the network. We introduce CanonQ, a unified quantization-aware training framework that addresses these challenges by separating source canonicalization from task-aware adaptation. Fixed rotations and energy normalization map heterogeneous tensor sources to canonical coordinates, enabling frozen Gaussian-reference codebooks to be reused across layers and models. Joint training then adapts the network to the coupled errors of weight, activation, and cache quantization within a common scalar/vector interface. We bound frozen-codebook transfer error and local task loss, and derive an exact normalization-aware straight-through Jacobian that links quantization distortion to gradient bias. The strongest gains arise under joint W2A4KV2 compression: across LLaMA3-1B/3B/8B, CanonQ-Omni achieves up to 14.28x lower WikiText-2 perplexity and up to 57.9% higher mean zero-shot accuracy than prior state-of-the-art and representative quantization baselines. The benefits extend to Qwen3-1.7B, code generation, and mathematical reasoning: on instruction-tuned MobileLLM-Pro-1B at W2A16KV16, CanonQ achieves relative improvements of 41.7% in HumanEval pass@1 and 39.1% in GSM8K exact match over the strongest evaluated quantization baseline.

</details>


### [66] [GeoNatureAgent (GNA): A Framework and Benchmark for Pre-Production Evaluation of Tool-Using Agents on Geospatial and Environmental Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.09112)
*Gabriel Diaz-Ireland,Diego Prieto-Herráez,Mario García Peces,Javier Velázquez,Benjamin Zaitchik,Devika Jain*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 GeoNatureAgent 框架与基准，通过固定 MCP 工具接口和确定性评分器，对九种 LLM 在 103 个地理空间任务上进行预生产评估，揭示能力、成本与评分严格性之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在部署前可靠评估工具调用型 LLM 智能体在真实 API 上的操作选择能力与成本效益

Method: GeoNatureAgent (GNA) 框架的核心是一个固定的十六工具地理空间 MCP 服务器，该服务器封装了实际的地理空间 API，提供统一工具层。被测智能体通过 MCP 接口调用这十六个工具，针对一个包含 103 个任务（93 个主任务、10 个比较任务）的基准套件执行操作。基准任务覆盖 18 个类别，基于一个开放的、可自托管的地理空间 API，该 API 服务西班牙和葡萄牙的三个环境指标。评估流程使用确定性评分器，对智能体的工具选择与操作结果进行评分。评测了九种 LLM，在温度 1.0 下使用三个随机种子进行多次运行，同时记录任务准确率与单例推理成本。评分方式包括严格全检查评分和逐检查部分信用评分。通过替换工具执行器和任务套件，该框架可实例化为任何地理空间领域的等效基准。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "任务准确率", "value": "61.7% ± 0.7%", "setting": "Claude Sonnet 4 在全部 103 个任务上，温度 1.0，三个种子"}, {"metric": "任务准确率", "value": "57.9%", "setting": "DeepSeek V3.2 在全部 103 个任务上"}, {"metric": "任务准确率", "value": "60.8%", "setting": "Claude Sonnet 4 在 93 个主任务上"}, {"metric": "成本-能力帕累托", "value": "DeepSeek V3.2 提供 Claude 93% 的能力，列表价格成本低 11.3 倍", "setting": "基于公开列表价格计算"}, {"metric": "严格全检查评分差距", "value": "最佳模型比通用 GIS 基准报告值低 24-36 分", "setting": "严格全检查评分 vs 通用 GIS 基准报告 85-97%"}, {"metric": "逐检查部分信用评分", "value": "前四名模型达到 86-90%", "setting": "逐检查部分信用评分"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 发布固定的十六工具地理空间 MCP 服务器接口，使被测智能体成为唯一变量；构建包含 103 个任务（93 主任务+10 比较任务）的基准，覆盖 18 个类别，基于自托管地理空间 API 服务西班牙和葡萄牙的三个环境指标；在三个温度 1.0 种子下评估九种 LLM，报告能力与单例成本作为正交维度；使用严格全检查评分与逐检查部分信用评分，揭示评分严格性对能力差距的影响

Abstract: Before tool-using LLM agents are deployed in environmental and geospatial workflows, teams need evidence that an agent reliably selects the right operations against real APIs. We introduce GeoNatureAgent (GNA), a framework for pre-production evaluation of tool-using agents: a fixed sixteen-tool geospatial interface published as a Model Context Protocol (MCP) server, so the agent under test is the only variable, scored against an identical tool layer, task suite, and deterministic scorer. Its flagship instance is a 103-task benchmark (a 93-task main suite across 18 categories plus a ten-task comparison expansion) evaluated against an open, self-hostable geospatial API serving three environmental indicators across Spain and Portugal. We evaluate nine LLMs under three temperature-1.0 seeds, reporting capability and per-case cost as orthogonal axes. (1) Claude Sonnet 4 achieves the highest capability (61.7% +/- 0.7% on all 103 tasks; 60.8% on the main suite), followed closely by DeepSeek V3.2 (57.9%), while no other model exceeds 53%; (2) the cost-accuracy Pareto frontier is mostly open-weight, with DeepSeek V3.2 offering 93% of Claude's capability at 11.3x lower list-price cost; (3) under strict all-checks scoring the best model sits 24-36 points below the 85-97% reported on general-purpose GIS benchmarks, whereas per-check partial credit for the top four models (86-90%) is comparable, so much of that gap reflects scoring strictness rather than task difficulty alone. The MCP server, evaluation harness, benchmark, and API are publicly available; swapping the tool executors and task suite instantiates an equivalent benchmark for any geospatial domain.

</details>


### [67] [RECAST: Learning to Compute the Right Context through Adaptive Evidence Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10507)
*Yilun Hao,Krishna Sayana,Isabella Ye,James S Ren,Sukhdeep Sodhi,Craig Boutilier,Chuchu Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RECAST 将 RAG 中的证据构建重新表述为在检索与计算操作上的序列决策：轻量级 RouterLM 用 SFT+GRPO 训练，迭代地选择或指定操作，交由冻结的 CompilerLM 生成可执行代码，证据充分后交给冻结的 AnswerLM 作答。六个基准族平均成功率 75.6%，超最强基线 15.9%；9B 的 RouterLM 经训练后超过免训练的 Gemini 3.5 Flash RouterLM 5.0%；三个留出基准平均提升 15.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 RAG 依赖固定相似度检索，agentic 变体仍以检索为中心，无法处理证据必须通过跨多个来源条目的过滤、聚合或计算才能推导出来的任务。

Method: RECAST 由三个角色组成：轻量级 RouterLM、冻结的 CompilerLM 和冻结的 AnswerLM。输入是长且异构的信息源（论文指出证据往往不存在于单一来源中）。处理流程是序列决策：RouterLM 在每一步迭代地选择并构造一个原始操作（primitive operation），或针对当前任务指定一个定制操作；这些操作由冻结的 CompilerLM 翻译为可执行代码并在信息源上执行。执行结果被 RouterLM 纳入上下文，RouterLM 据此决定继续选择操作还是终止。当 RouterLM 判断已收集到的证据足以支撑求解时，它把“被接受的证据”传递给冻结的 AnswerLM，由 AnswerLM 生成最终答案。训练目标：RouterLM 先做监督微调（SFT），再用组相对策略优化（GRPO）优化，使其学会在检索与计算之间做路由并判断证据充分性。推理时仅 RouterLM 需要被调用并迭代，CompilerLM 与 AnswerLM 保持冻结，因此可通过复用现成大模型降低训练成本。该设计解决的问题是：把证据从“被检索到的文本”升级为“跨来源计算出的结果”，并用可执行代码保证过滤、聚合、计算类操作的正确执行。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "平均成功率", "value": "75.6%", "setting": "六个异构基准族"}, {"metric": "相对最强基线提升", "value": "+15.9%", "setting": "六个异构基准族，与最强大型模型基线对比"}, {"metric": "成功率优势", "value": "+5.0%", "setting": "Qwen3.5-9B RouterLM（经训练）对比免训练的 Gemini 3.5 Flash RouterLM"}, {"metric": "相对最强基线平均提升", "value": "+15.0%", "setting": "三个留出基准，零样本泛化"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 把证据构建形式化为在异构检索与计算操作上的序列决策过程，实现“主动推导证据”而非“仅检索证据”；轻量级 RouterLM 迭代选择并构造原始操作，或指定定制操作，交由冻结的 CompilerLM 翻译成可执行代码；RouterLM 自主判断证据是否充分，充分后交给冻结的 AnswerLM 生成最终答案；RouterLM 采用 SFT 后接 GRPO 的训练方式，使小模型在路由决策上超过免训练的大模型

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly applied to tasks grounded in long, heterogeneous information sources. Conventional Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) relies on fixed similarity-based retrieval, while agentic variants adapt queries and tool use but remain largely retrieval-centric. However, in many tasks, the evidence required for a solution is not explicitly present in any single source item. Instead, it must be derived through filtering, aggregation, or computation across multiple source items. In this work, we introduce RECAST (Routing Evidence through Computation, Access, and Synthesized Tools), a learned framework that formulates evidence construction as a sequential decision process over heterogeneous retrieval and computation operations, allowing evidence to be actively derived rather than merely retrieved. A lightweight RouterLM iteratively selects and formulates primitive operations or specifies customized operations for a frozen CompilerLM to translate into executable code. Once it judges the evidence sufficient, RouterLM passes the accepted evidence to a frozen AnswerLM to produce the final solution. We train RouterLM with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) followed by group relative policy optimization (GRPO). Across six heterogeneous benchmark families, RECAST achieves a mean success rate of 75.6%, outperforming the strongest large-model baseline by 15.9%. Moreover, training enables the Qwen3.5-9B RouterLM to outperform a training-free Gemini 3.5 Flash RouterLM by 5.0%. On three held-out benchmarks, RECAST improves over the strongest baseline by 15.0% on average, demonstrating strong zero-shot generalization across tasks and heterogeneous source representations.

</details>


### [68] [Before They Can Solve: Predicting Post-Training Coding-Agent Performance from Base Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.10478)
*Tan Yu,Alexander Bukharin,Khushi Bhardwaj,Jennifer Williams,Zirui Liu,Jonathan Lingjie Li,Soumye Singhal,Joseph Jennings,Sanjeev Satheesh,Yash Jain,Ashish Vaswani,Venkat Krishna Srinivasan,Matthew Papakipos,Hyunwoo Kim,Jian Zhang,Oleksii Kuchaiev,Markus Kliegl,Mostofa Patwary,Mohammad Shoeybi,Bryan Catanzaro,Jonathan Cohen,Jiantao Jiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出利用后训练智能体成功轨迹作为前瞻信号，通过三种筛选（决定性动作BPB、Patch MCQ、前缀条件pass@K）预测基础模型在智能体编码任务上的后训练性能，在十个模型对上与后训练SWE-bench Verified pass@1排名高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 如何在不运行完整智能体后训练的情况下，预测基础检查点在多步工具使用编码任务上的后训练性能。

Method: 该方法首先收集针对特定编码基准（如SWE-bench）的后训练智能体成功轨迹。对于每条轨迹，系统重放该轨迹，并在每个会修改代码的步骤之后重新运行测试套件，以确定‘决定性步骤’——即该步骤的累积补丁首次使仓库从失败状态翻转为通过状态，从而证明记录的动作在给定前序上下文的条件下解决了任务。随后，在决定性步骤处应用三种筛选：(i) 决定性动作每字节比特数（BPB）：测量基础检查点在认证动作上的概率质量；(ii) 补丁多项选择（Patch MCQ）：测试检查点在该认证动作与由同一验证器拒绝的替代动作之间的选择倾向；(iii) 前缀条件pass@K：评估对功能正确生成的支持度，并给予任何测试接受的续写以学分。这些筛选仅需基准的成功轨迹和验证器，无需基础检查点冷启动驱动完整测试框架。最终，根据这些筛选得分对候选基础检查点进行排序，预测它们在经过完整后训练后达到的pass@1性能。

Result: {"offline_gains": [], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将成功的后训练智能体轨迹视为基础模型潜力的前瞻信号；重放轨迹并在每个代码更改步骤后运行测试，识别决定性的步骤（首次翻转失败到通过的步骤）；基于决定性步骤构建三种无需冷启动驱动的筛选：决定性动作BPB、Patch MCQ、前缀条件pass@K

Abstract: How can we predict which base checkpoint is worth an expensive round of agentic post-training? End-to-end pass@$K$ tests whether successful behavior already appears in a base model's distribution, but it is a poor fit for agentic coding: many base checkpoints cannot reliably produce the well-formed tool invocation required to complete a task end-to-end. Single-shot or short-horizon tasks avoid these tool-calling failures by collapsing a multi-step interaction into a fixed prompt and a single patch, but they sidestep the core capability we care about: maintaining coherent state over many tool-using steps as the repository evolves. To bridge this gap, we treat successful post-trained agent trajectories as a lookahead signal of base-model potential. Replaying each trajectory and rerunning tests after every code-changing step identifies the decisive step: the first step whose cumulative patch flips the repository from failing to passing, certifying that the recorded action solves the task given the prior context. Motivated by a coverage principle for agentic traces, we build three screens at this step that do not require a base checkpoint to drive the harness from a cold start: (i) Decisive-Action BPB (bits per byte) measures the probability mass on the certified action, (ii) Patch MCQ tests the checkpoint's choice between that action and alternatives rejected by the same verifier, and (iii) prefix-conditioned pass@$K$ evaluates support for functionally-correct generations and credits any continuation that the tests accept. Across ten pairs of public base and post-trained models, all three screens rank the cohort in close agreement with post-trained SWE-bench Verified pass@$1$. As our methods need only a benchmark's successful trajectories and its verifier, they can be applied to turn future agentic coding benchmarks into base-model evaluations.

</details>

*Haonan Deng,Park Sinchaisri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出智能体个人记忆评估只看向生成结果会掩盖生成前的过时、错误归因和缺失问题，提出在生成前直接测量记忆构建与检索失败，并发现时间有效性更多取决于记忆构建，身份解析仍难区分同名说话者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个人记忆智能体仅以最终答案正确性评判，掩盖了生成前的记忆层错误：过时值暴露、错误人物归因、截止前无用事实；需要直接测量生成前记忆失败。

Method: 论文以Personal Fact Memory (PFM)为参考层，构建一个生成前的记忆评估管道。输入为对话历史或记忆库中存储的事实槽位与对应值；记忆构建模块负责将修订（revision）分配给正确槽位，输出活跃值存储。检索器在相同活跃存储和参与者信息下进行检索，参与者感知BM25与参考排序器在预设0.02边界内等价。排序后进入身份解析：实体后验用于减少同名暴露，但对完全同名说话者无法区分。最后将过滤后的记忆块送入两个冻结语言模型生成文本，以复现提示错误。整个流程在受控修订基准、LongMemEval和LoCoMo上评估，指标包括过时暴露率、干净检索率、合并召回、同名暴露和延迟。检索延迟因排序器而异，但prompt预填充主导单轮延迟。

Result: {"offline_gains": [{"metric": "过时值暴露率", "value": "70.3% prompts expose a superseded value", "setting": "无更新解析的控制修订基准"}, {"metric": "过时暴露", "value": "serving only active value eliminates observed stale exposure", "setting": "正确键控槽位，仅服务活跃值"}, {"metric": "开放域合并召回", "value": "never exceeds 0.062", "setting": "LongMemEval"}], "online_gains": []}

Conclusion: 将时间有效性归因于记忆构建阶段，发现仅服务活跃值即可消除观察到的过时暴露；发现参与者感知BM25与参考排序器在预设0.02边界内等价；揭示LLM键分配器键召回高但干净检索率低，且长程合并召回不超过0.062；实体后验可减少同名暴露但无法区分完全同名说话者；用两个冻结语言模型复现生成文本中的提示错误

Abstract: Personal memory for language agents is usually judged by whether the final an- swer is correct. That score hides errors that arise before generation: the memory block may contain an obsolete value, a fact about the wrong person, or no use- ful fact before the serving deadline. We measure these failures directly. Using Personal Fact Memory (PFM) as a reference layer, we find that temporal validity is primarily a property of memory construction in our setting. On a controlled revision benchmark, serving only the active value of each correctly keyed slot eliminates observed stale exposure; without update resolution, 70.3% of prompts expose a superseded value. Once retrievers share the same active store and par- ticipant information, participant-aware BM25 is equivalent to the reference ranker within a prespecified 0.02 margin. The harder problem is assigning revisions to the right slot. Missed merges leave stale values active, whereas false merges silently remove current values; four LLM key assigners achieve higher key re- call than a rule extractor yet produce lower clean-retrieval rates, and open-domain merge recall on LongMemEval never exceeds 0.062. Misattribution survives va- lidity filtering: an entity posterior reduces same-name exposure on controlled data but cannot distinguish identically named speakers in LoCoMo. Two frozen lan- guage models reproduce prompt errors in generated text. Retrieval latency varies across rankers, but prompt prefill dominates turn-level latency on our hardware. These results argue for evaluating agent memory before generation, separating stored-state validity, identity resolution, abstention, and serving latency.

</details>
